import com.google.gms.googleservices.GoogleServicesPlugin.MissingGoogleServicesStrategy

plugins {
  alias(libs.plugins.android.application)
  alias(libs.plugins.kotlin.compose)
  alias(libs.plugins.google.devtools.ksp)
  alias(libs.plugins.secrets)
  alias(libs.plugins.google.services)
}

android {
  namespace = "com.example"
  compileSdk { version = release(36) { minorApiLevel = 1 } }

  defaultConfig {
    applicationId = "com.aistudio.mamanbebe.trckrx"
    minSdk = 26
    targetSdk = 36
    versionCode = 1
    versionName = "1.0.0"

    testInstrumentationRunner = "androidx.test.runner.AndroidJUnitRunner"
  }

  // Do not remove or modify these signingConfigs. They are necessary for building, installing,
  // and updating Android apps in AI Studio.
  signingConfigs {
    create("release") {
      val keystorePath = System.getenv("KEYSTORE_PATH") ?: "${rootDir}/my-upload-key.jks"
      storeFile = file(keystorePath)
      storePassword = System.getenv("STORE_PASSWORD")
      keyAlias = "upload"
      keyPassword = System.getenv("KEY_PASSWORD")
    }
    create("debugConfig") {
      storeFile = file("${rootDir}/debug.keystore")
      storePassword = "android"
      keyAlias = "androiddebugkey"
      keyPassword = "android"
    }
  }

  buildTypes {
    release {
      isCrunchPngs = false
      isMinifyEnabled = false
      proguardFiles(getDefaultProguardFile("proguard-android-optimize.txt"), "proguard-rules.pro")
      // Do not remove or modify this signingConfig assignment. It is necessary for Android apps in
      // AI Studio.
      signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("release")
    }
    debug {
      // Do not remove or modify this signingConfig assignment. It is necessary for Android apps in
      // AI Studio.
      signingConfig = signingConfigs.getByName("debugConfig")
    }
  }
  compileOptions {
    sourceCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
    targetCompatibility = JavaVersion.VERSION_17
  }
  buildFeatures {
    compose = true
    buildConfig = true
  }
  testOptions { unitTests { isIncludeAndroidResources = true } }
  dependenciesInfo {
    includeInApk = false
    includeInBundle = true
  }
}

// Configure the Secrets Gradle Plugin to use .env and .env.example files
// to match the convention used in Web projects.
secrets {
  propertiesFileName = ".env"
  defaultPropertiesFileName = ".env.example"
  ignoreList.add("FIREBASE_APPCHECK_DEBUG_TOKEN")
}

googleServices { missingGoogleServicesStrategy = MissingGoogleServicesStrategy.WARN }

package com.example

import androidx.test.ext.junit.runners.AndroidJUnit4
import androidx.test.platform.app.InstrumentationRegistry
import org.junit.Assert.*
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith

/**
 * Instrumented test, which will execute on an Android device.
 *
 * See [testing documentation](http://d.android.com/tools/testing).
 */
@RunWith(AndroidJUnit4::class)
class ExampleInstrumentedTest {
  @Test
  fun useAppContext() {
    // Context of the app under test.
    val appContext = InstrumentationRegistry.getInstrumentation().targetContext
    assertEquals(BuildConfig.APPLICATION_ID, appContext.packageName)
  }
}

<?xml version="1.0" encoding="utf-8"?>
<manifest xmlns:android="http://schemas.android.com/apk/res/android"
    xmlns:tools="http://schemas.android.com/tools">

    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.INTERNET" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.ACCESS_NETWORK_STATE" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.VIBRATE" />

    <!-- Health Connect Permissions for pregnancy weight, hydration, and sleep -->
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.health.READ_WEIGHT" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.health.WRITE_WEIGHT" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.health.READ_SLEEP" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.health.WRITE_SLEEP" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.health.READ_HYDRATION" />
    <uses-permission android:name="android.permission.health.WRITE_HYDRATION" />

    <queries>
        <package android:name="com.google.android.apps.healthdata" />
        <intent>
            <action android:name="androidx.health.ACTION_SHOW_ONBOARDING" />
        </intent>
    </queries>

    <application
        android:allowBackup="true"
        android:dataExtractionRules="@xml/data_extraction_rules"
        android:fullBackupContent="@xml/backup_rules"
        android:icon="@mipmap/ic_launcher"
        android:label="@string/app_name"
        android:roundIcon="@mipmap/ic_launcher_round"
        android:supportsRtl="true"
        android:theme="@style/Theme.MyApplication">
        <activity
            android:name=".MainActivity"
            android:exported="true"
            android:label="@string/app_name"
            android:theme="@style/Theme.MyApplication"
            android:windowSoftInputMode="adjustResize">
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.MAIN" />

                <category android:name="android.intent.category.LAUNCHER" />
            </intent-filter>
            <!-- Health Connect rationale flow -->
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="androidx.health.ACTION_SHOW_PERMISSIONS_RATIONALE" />
            </intent-filter>
        </activity>

        <!-- Health Connect permissions rationale alias for Android 14+ platform -->
        <activity-alias
            android:name=".ViewPermissionUsageActivity"
            android:exported="true"
            android:targetActivity=".MainActivity"
            android:permission="android.permission.START_VIEW_PERMISSION_USAGE">
            <intent-filter>
                <action android:name="android.intent.action.VIEW_PERMISSION_USAGE" />
                <category android:name="android.intent.category.HEALTH_PERMISSIONS" />
            </intent-filter>
        </activity-alias>

        <!-- FileProvider for sharing exported medical PDF documents -->
        <provider
            android:name="androidx.core.content.FileProvider"
            android:authorities="${applicationId}.fileprovider"
            android:exported="false"
            android:grantUriPermissions="true">
            <meta-data
                android:name="android.support.FILE_PROVIDER_PATHS"
                android:resource="@xml/file_paths" />
        </provider>
    </application>

</manifest>

package com.example

import android.content.res.Configuration
import android.os.Bundle
import androidx.activity.ComponentActivity
import androidx.activity.compose.BackHandler
import androidx.activity.compose.setContent
import androidx.activity.enableEdgeToEdge
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.Box
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.fillMaxSize
import androidx.compose.foundation.layout.padding
import androidx.compose.material3.Scaffold
import androidx.compose.runtime.Composable
import androidx.compose.runtime.CompositionLocalProvider
import androidx.compose.runtime.LaunchedEffect
import androidx.compose.runtime.collectAsState
import androidx.compose.runtime.getValue
import androidx.compose.runtime.mutableStateOf
import androidx.compose.runtime.remember
import androidx.compose.runtime.setValue
import androidx.compose.ui.Modifier
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalConfiguration
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalContext
import androidx.compose.ui.platform.LocalLayoutDirection
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.LayoutDirection
import androidx.compose.ui.unit.dp
import androidx.lifecycle.viewmodel.compose.viewModel
import com.example.ui.components.LanguageSelectorDialog
import com.example.ui.components.MamanBottomBar
import com.example.ui.components.MamanTopBar
import com.example.ui.components.PinLockScreen
import com.example.ui.components.SettingsPrivacyDialog
import com.example.ui.components.UserProfileDialog
import com.example.ui.screens.AppointmentsScreen
import com.example.ui.screens.BabyLogScreen
import com.example.ui.screens.ChecklistScreen
import com.example.ui.screens.HealthConnectDialog
import com.example.ui.screens.KickCounterScreen
import com.example.ui.screens.MamanAssistantScreen
import com.example.ui.screens.MilestoneJournalScreen
import com.example.ui.screens.PregnancyLogScreen
import com.example.ui.screens.TodayScreen
import com.example.ui.screens.WeekDetailScreen
import com.example.ui.theme.MamanBebeTheme
import com.example.ui.viewmodel.PregnancyViewModel
import com.example.ui.viewmodel.ScreenTab
import java.util.Locale

class MainActivity : ComponentActivity() {
    override fun onCreate(savedInstanceState: Bundle?) {
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState)
        enableEdgeToEdge()
        com.example.repository.PregnancySyncWorker.scheduleSync(this)
        setContent {
            MamanBebeTheme {
                MamanBebeApp()
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun MamanBebeApp(
    viewModel: PregnancyViewModel = viewModel()
) {
    val currentTab by viewModel.currentTab.collectAsState()
    val currentWeek by viewModel.currentWeekNumber.collectAsState()
    val hasHealthPerms by viewModel.hasHealthPermissions.collectAsState()
    val currentLanguage by viewModel.currentLanguage.collectAsState()
    val isPinEnabled by viewModel.isPinEnabled.collectAsState()
    val isAppLocked by viewModel.isAppLocked.collectAsState()
    val profileName by viewModel.profileName.collectAsState()
    val profileAge by viewModel.profileAge.collectAsState()
    val profilePhotoUri by viewModel.profilePhotoUri.collectAsState()
    val titleColorHex by viewModel.titleColorHex.collectAsState()

    var isShowingWeekDetail by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showHealthConnectDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showLanguageDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showSettingsDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showProfileDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val locale = remember(currentLanguage) {
        when (currentLanguage) {
            "fr" -> Locale.FRENCH
            else -> Locale.ENGLISH
        }
    }

    val baseContext = LocalContext.current
    val baseConfig = LocalConfiguration.current

    val localizedConfig = remember(locale, baseConfig) {
        Configuration(baseConfig).apply {
            setLocale(locale)
            setLayoutDirection(locale)
        }
    }

    val localizedContext = remember(locale, baseContext) {
        val conf = Configuration(baseContext.resources.configuration).apply {
            setLocale(locale)
            setLayoutDirection(locale)
        }
        val configContext = baseContext.createConfigurationContext(conf)
        object : android.content.ContextWrapper(baseContext) {
            override fun getResources(): android.content.res.Resources = configContext.resources
        }
    }

    LaunchedEffect(locale) {
        Locale.setDefault(locale)
        try {
            val appLocale = androidx.core.os.LocaleListCompat.forLanguageTags(currentLanguage)
            androidx.appcompat.app.AppCompatDelegate.setApplicationLocales(appLocale)
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    val layoutDirection = if (currentLanguage == "ar") LayoutDirection.Rtl else LayoutDirection.Ltr
    val activityResultRegistryOwner = androidx.activity.compose.LocalActivityResultRegistryOwner.current
        ?: (baseContext as? androidx.activity.result.ActivityResultRegistryOwner)
    val backPressedDispatcherOwner = androidx.activity.compose.LocalOnBackPressedDispatcherOwner.current
        ?: (baseContext as? androidx.activity.OnBackPressedDispatcherOwner)

    val locals = buildList {
        add(LocalConfiguration provides localizedConfig)
        add(LocalContext provides localizedContext)
        add(LocalLayoutDirection provides layoutDirection)
        if (activityResultRegistryOwner != null) {
            add(androidx.activity.compose.LocalActivityResultRegistryOwner provides activityResultRegistryOwner)
        }
        if (backPressedDispatcherOwner != null) {
            add(androidx.activity.compose.LocalOnBackPressedDispatcherOwner provides backPressedDispatcherOwner)
        }
    }

    CompositionLocalProvider(*locals.toTypedArray()) {
        if (isPinEnabled && isAppLocked) {
            PinLockScreen(
                viewModel = viewModel
            )
            return@CompositionLocalProvider
        }

        BackHandler(enabled = isShowingWeekDetail || currentTab != ScreenTab.TODAY) {
            if (isShowingWeekDetail) {
                isShowingWeekDetail = false
            } else {
                viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.TODAY)
            }
        }

        Scaffold(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
            topBar = {
                if (!isShowingWeekDetail) {
                    MamanTopBar(
                        currentWeek = currentWeek,
                        currentTab = currentTab,
                        profileName = profileName,
                        profileAge = profileAge,
                        profilePhotoUri = profilePhotoUri,
                        titleColorHex = titleColorHex,
                        onWeekClick = { isShowingWeekDetail = true },
                        onProfileClick = { showProfileDialog = true },
                        onSettingsClick = { showSettingsDialog = true }
                    )
                }
            },
            bottomBar = {
                if (!isShowingWeekDetail) {
                    MamanBottomBar(
                        currentTab = currentTab,
                        onTabSelected = { tab ->
                            isShowingWeekDetail = false
                            viewModel.selectTab(tab)
                        }
                    )
                }
            }
        ) { innerPadding ->
            val effectiveBottomPadding = if (currentTab == ScreenTab.ASSISTANT) 0.dp else innerPadding.calculateBottomPadding()
            val effectiveTopPadding = innerPadding.calculateTopPadding()
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .padding(
                        top = effectiveTopPadding,
                        bottom = effectiveBottomPadding
                    )
            ) {
                if (isShowingWeekDetail) {
                    WeekDetailScreen(
                        viewModel = viewModel,
                        onBack = { isShowingWeekDetail = false }
                    )
                } else {
                    when (currentTab) {
                        ScreenTab.TODAY -> TodayScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            onNavigateWeekDetail = { isShowingWeekDetail = true },
                            onOpenHealthConnectDialog = { showHealthConnectDialog = true },
                            onOpenLanguageDialog = { showLanguageDialog = true },
                            onOpenSettings = { showSettingsDialog = true }
                        )
                        ScreenTab.STATS -> com.example.ui.screens.StatsScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            onOpenHealthConnectDialog = { showHealthConnectDialog = true }
                        )
                        ScreenTab.KICK_COUNTER -> KickCounterScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel
                        )
                        ScreenTab.HEALTH_LOG -> PregnancyLogScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            onOpenHealthConnectDialog = { showHealthConnectDialog = true },
                            onOpenLanguageDialog = { showLanguageDialog = true }
                        )
                        ScreenTab.BABY_LOG -> BabyLogScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel
                        )
                        ScreenTab.ASSISTANT -> MamanAssistantScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            bottomBarPadding = innerPadding.calculateBottomPadding(),
                            onOpenLanguageDialog = { showLanguageDialog = true },
                            onBack = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.TODAY) }
                        )
                        ScreenTab.JOURNAL -> MilestoneJournalScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            onBack = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.TODAY) }
                        )
                        ScreenTab.CHECKLIST -> ChecklistScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            onBack = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.TODAY) }
                        )
                        ScreenTab.AGENDA -> AppointmentsScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            onBack = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.TODAY) }
                        )
                        ScreenTab.ROADMAP -> com.example.ui.screens.RoadmapScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            onBack = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.TODAY) }
                        )
                        ScreenTab.KNOWLEDGE, ScreenTab.FERTILITY -> com.example.ui.screens.KnowledgeScreen(
                            viewModel = viewModel,
                            onBack = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.TODAY) }
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (showHealthConnectDialog) {
            HealthConnectDialog(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                onDismiss = { showHealthConnectDialog = false }
            )
        }

        if (showLanguageDialog) {
            LanguageSelectorDialog(
                currentLanguageCode = currentLanguage,
                onLanguageSelected = { langCode ->
                    viewModel.setLanguage(langCode)
                },
                onDismiss = { showLanguageDialog = false }
            )
        }

        if (showSettingsDialog) {
            SettingsPrivacyDialog(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                onDismiss = { showSettingsDialog = false }
            )
        }

        if (showProfileDialog) {
            UserProfileDialog(
                viewModel = viewModel,
                onDismiss = { showProfileDialog = false }
            )
        }
    }
}

FILE 6/77: app/src/main/java/com/example/ai/GeminiAssistantClient.kt
================================================================================

package com.example.ai

import com.example.BuildConfig
import com.google.ai.client.generativeai.GenerativeModel
import com.google.ai.client.generativeai.type.content
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

class GeminiAssistantClient {

    private val systemInstruction = """
        You are 'Maman Assistant', an empathetic, clinically sound pregnancy and postpartum companion.
        Always follow reassuring, evidence-based obstetric guidelines (ACOG / WHO / RCOG).
        Provide clear, warm, actionable guidance, reminding mothers to consult their OB/GYN, midwife, or care team for urgent symptoms.
        Format your answers with clean paragraphs, supportive tone, and helpful tips.
    """.trimIndent()

    private val generativeModel by lazy {
        val apiKey = try {
            BuildConfig.GEMINI_API_KEY
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            ""
        }

        if (apiKey.isNotBlank() && apiKey != "MY_GEMINI_API_KEY") {
            GenerativeModel(
                modelName = "gemini-2.5-flash",
                apiKey = apiKey,
                systemInstruction = content { text(systemInstruction) }
            )
        } else {
            null
        }
    }

    suspend fun askAssistant(
        prompt: String,
        currentWeek: Int? = null,
        userLanguage: String = "en"
    ): Result<String> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val model = generativeModel
        if (model != null) {
            try {
                val contextualPrompt = buildString {
                    if (currentWeek != null) {
                        append("[Context: Mother is currently at Pregnancy Week $currentWeek, preferred language: $userLanguage]\n\n")
                    }
                    append(prompt)
                }
                val response = model.generateContent(contextualPrompt)
                val reply = response.text?.trim()
                if (!reply.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    return@withContext Result.success(reply)
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                // If API call throws (e.g. invalid key or network issue), provide smart obstetric fallback
                return@withContext Result.success(getCuratedObstetricGuidance(prompt, currentWeek))
            }
        }

        // Curated, immediate clinical fallback when API key is pending in Secrets panel
        Result.success(getCuratedObstetricGuidance(prompt, currentWeek))
    }

    private fun getCuratedObstetricGuidance(prompt: String, currentWeek: Int?): String {
        val query = prompt.lowercase()
        val weekContext = if (currentWeek != null) "At Week $currentWeek of your pregnancy, " else ""

        return when {
            query.contains("heartburn") || query.contains("acid") || query.contains("reflux") -> {
                """
                ${weekContext}heartburn is very common due to progesterone relaxing the esophageal sphincter, plus the growing baby gently nudging your stomach upward.

                💡 Practical tips to find relief:
                • Eat smaller, more frequent meals rather than large portions.
                • Stay upright for at least 45–60 minutes after eating.
                • Avoid known triggers like citrus, spicy foods, caffeine, and heavy fried meals.
                • Sip ginger or chamomile tea, or cold milk.
                • Elevate your head and torso slightly with a wedge pillow at bedtime.

                ⚠️ Consult your doctor or midwife before starting calcium antacids or medications.
                """.trimIndent()
            }
            query.contains("kick") || query.contains("movement") || query.contains("flutter") -> {
                """
                ${weekContext}monitoring fetal movements provides great reassurance about your baby's wellbeing!

                🩺 Clinical Kick Counting Guidelines (ACOG):
                • Best time: After a snack or meal, or when relaxing on your left side in the evening.
                • Goal: You should feel 10 distinct movements (kicks, rolls, swishes, or flutters) within 2 hours.
                • Most babies reach 10 kicks in less than 30 minutes when active.

                ⚠️ If you notice a sudden decrease in movements or have not felt 10 kicks within 2 hours after having a cold drink and resting on your side, contact your maternity triage or OB/GYN immediately.
                """.trimIndent()
            }
            query.contains("exercise") || query.contains("stretch") || query.contains("workout") -> {
                """
                ${weekContext}staying active offers wonderful benefits for stamina, mood, and preparing for labor!

                🧘 Safe Pregnancy Activities:
                • Walking, prenatal yoga, stationary cycling, and swimming (which takes weight off your joints!).
                • Gentle cat-cow stretches and pelvic tilts to relieve lower back tension.
                • Pelvic floor (Kegel) conditioning exercises.

                ⛔ Avoid activities with fall risks, contact sports, or lying flat on your back for prolonged periods after 20 weeks. Always stay well-hydrated.
                """.trimIndent()
            }
            query.contains("bag") || query.contains("pack") || query.contains("hospital") -> {
                """
                Packing your hospital bag around Weeks 34–36 brings wonderful peace of mind!

                🎒 Essentials for Mom:
                • Photo ID, insurance card, medical records, and birth preferences plan.
                • 2–3 pairs of loose, dark nursing pajamas or gowns with easy front access.
                • Cozy non-skid socks, warm slippers, and lip balm.
                • Extra-long phone charger cable.
                • High-waisted postpartum underwear and maternity pads.

                👶 Essentials for Baby:
                • Installed infant car seat (inspected!).
                • 2 going-home outfits (Newborn and 0–3M sizes).
                • Soft swaddle blanket, hat, and scratch mittens.
                """.trimIndent()
            }
            query.contains("colic") || query.contains("cry") || query.contains("soothe") || query.contains("nursing") -> {
                """
                Soothing a newborn can test your patience, but gentle comfort techniques work wonders!

                👶 The 5 S's Technique (Dr. Harvey Karp):
                • Swaddle: A snug swaddle creates the cozy security of the womb.
                • Side or Stomach position: Hold baby on their side or across your forearm while awake and supervised.
                • Shushing: Gentle white noise or rhythmic 'shhh' sounds louder than their crying.
                • Swinging: Gentle, rhythmic swaying or rocking motions.
                • Sucking: Pacifier or nursing latch for non-nutritive soothing.

                Remember to take turns with your partner when you feel overwhelmed. You are doing an incredible job!
                """.trimIndent()
            }
            query.contains("food") || query.contains("eat") || query.contains("nutrition") || query.contains("diet") -> {
                """
                ${weekContext}nourishing your body with wholesome nutrition supports your baby's rapid growth.

                🥗 Focus on:
                • Protein: Eggs, lentils, tofu, well-cooked poultry, and Greek yogurt for tissue building.
                • Iron & Folate: Dark leafy greens, fortified cereals, and lean meats to support higher blood volume.
                • Choline & Omega-3 (DHA): Eggs and low-mercury cooked fish (salmon) for fetal brain development.
                • Plenty of water: Aim for 2.5 to 3 liters daily.

                🚫 Foods to avoid: Raw/undercooked meats and sushi, unpasteurized cheeses, deli meats unless heated to steaming, and excess caffeine (>200mg/day).
                """.trimIndent()
            }
            else -> {
                """
                ${weekContext}I'm here as your Maman Assistant companion. 

                Every pregnancy journey is unique. Your body is undergoing remarkable transformations as your baby grows each day. 

                Remember to listen closely to your body, stay gently hydrated, and rest whenever fatigue sets in. If you have questions about specific symptoms (like backaches, kicks, Braxton Hicks, or tests), feel free to ask!

                👩‍⚕️ For symptoms like sudden severe swelling, persistent severe headache, visual disturbances, or vaginal bleeding, contact your healthcare provider right away.
                """.trimIndent()
            }
        }
    }
}



================================================================================
FILE 7/77: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/CachedContact.kt
================================================================================

package com.example.data

import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey
import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.Update
import androidx.room.Delete
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.Query
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Entity(tableName = "cached_contacts")
data class CachedContact(
    @PrimaryKey val id: String,
    val type: String, // "DOCTOR", "PHARMACY", or "CLINIC"
    val name: String,
    val phone: String?,
    val address: String? = "",
    val notes: String? = ""
)

@Dao
interface ContactDao {
    @Query("SELECT * FROM cached_contacts WHERE type = :contactType ORDER BY name ASC")
    fun getContactsByType(contactType: String): Flow<List<CachedContact>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM cached_contacts ORDER BY name ASC")
    fun getAllCachedContacts(): Flow<List<CachedContact>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertContacts(contacts: List<CachedContact>)

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertSingleContact(contact: CachedContact)

    @Update
    suspend fun updateContact(contact: CachedContact)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteContact(contact: CachedContact)

    @Query("DELETE FROM cached_contacts WHERE type = :contactType")
    suspend fun clearContactsByType(contactType: String)
}

                • 5-1-1 Rule: Contractions occurring every 5 minutes, lasting 1 full minute, continuing for 1 hour.
                • Water breaking: Clear fluid release (trickle or gush); note the time, color, and odor.

    fun getWeek(weekNumber: Int): PregnancyWeek {
        val clamped = weekNumber.coerceIn(1, 42)
        return weeks.minByOrNull { kotlin.math.abs(it.weekNumber - clamped) } ?: weeks.first()
    }

FILE 11/77: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/local/AppDatabase.kt
================================================================================

package com.example.data.local

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import androidx.room.Database
import androidx.room.Room
import androidx.room.RoomDatabase

@Database(
    entities = [
        PregnancyLogEntity::class,
        KickSessionEntity::class,
        BabyLogEntity::class,
        JournalEntryEntity::class,
        AppointmentEntity::class,
        ChecklistItemEntity::class,
        MedicalContactEntity::class,
        com.example.data.CachedContact::class
    ],
    version = 5,
    exportSchema = false
)
abstract class AppDatabase : RoomDatabase() {
    abstract fun pregnancyDao(): PregnancyDao
    abstract fun contactDao(): com.example.data.ContactDao

    companion object {
        private const val TAG = "AppDatabase"
        private const val DB_NAME = "maman_bebe_database"

        @Volatile
        private var INSTANCE: AppDatabase? = null

        fun getInstance(context: Context): AppDatabase {
            return INSTANCE ?: synchronized(this) {
                val existing = INSTANCE
                if (existing != null) return@synchronized existing

                val appContext = context.applicationContext
                val db = try {
                    val created = buildDatabase(appContext)
                    // Trigger database opening immediately inside try-catch to catch schema verification errors
                    created.openHelper.writableDatabase
                    created
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.e(TAG, "Legacy database schema mismatch or corruption detected: ${e.message}. Rebuilding cleanly.", e)
                    try {
                        appContext.deleteDatabase(DB_NAME)
                    } catch (delEx: Exception) {
                        Log.e(TAG, "Failed to delete corrupted database: ${delEx.message}")
                    }
                    val freshDb = buildDatabase(appContext)
                    try {
                        freshDb.openHelper.writableDatabase
                    } catch (_: Exception) {}
                    freshDb
                }
                INSTANCE = db
                db
            }
        }

        private fun buildDatabase(context: Context): AppDatabase {
            return Room.databaseBuilder(
                context,
                AppDatabase::class.java,
                DB_NAME
            )
                .fallbackToDestructiveMigration(true)
                .fallbackToDestructiveMigrationOnDowngrade(true)
                .build()
        }
    }
}

package com.example.data.local

import androidx.room.Entity
import androidx.room.PrimaryKey

@Entity(tableName = "pregnancy_logs")
data class PregnancyLogEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val dateString: String,
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val weightKg: Double = 0.0,
    val waterGlasses: Int = 0,
    val mood: String = "Calm",
    val symptoms: String = "",
    val bloodPressure: String = "",
    val notes: String = "",
    val syncedHealthConnect: Boolean = false
)

@Entity(tableName = "kick_sessions")
data class KickSessionEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val durationSeconds: Long = 0,
    val kickCount: Int = 10,
    val targetReached: Boolean = true,
    val notes: String = ""
)

@Entity(tableName = "baby_logs")
data class BabyLogEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val logType: String, // NURSING, BOTTLE, DIAPER, SLEEP
    val detail: String, // LEFT / RIGHT / BOTH / WET / DIRTY / FORMULA
    val durationSeconds: Long = 0,
    val amountMl: Int = 0,
    val notes: String = "",
    val syncedHealthConnect: Boolean = false
)

@Entity(tableName = "journal_entries")
data class JournalEntryEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val weekNumber: Int,
    val dateString: String,
    val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val title: String,
    val reflection: String,
    val photoUri: String? = null,
    val category: String = "Belly Bump"
)

@Entity(tableName = "appointments")
data class AppointmentEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val title: String,
    val weekSuggested: Int = 0,
    val dateTimestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis(),
    val doctorName: String = "",
    val location: String = "",
    val notes: String = "",
    val isCompleted: Boolean = false
)

@Entity(tableName = "checklist_items")
data class ChecklistItemEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val category: String,
    val title: String,
    val isChecked: Boolean = false,
    val isCustom: Boolean = false
)

@Entity(tableName = "medical_contacts")
data class MedicalContactEntity(
    @PrimaryKey(autoGenerate = true) val id: Long = 0,
    val type: String, // "DOCTOR", "PHARMACY", "CLINIC", "HOSPITAL"
    val name: String,
    val phone: String = "",
    val address: String = "",
    val notes: String = ""
)

FILE 13/77: app/src/main/java/com/example/data/local/PregnancyDao.kt

package com.example.data.local

import androidx.room.Dao
import androidx.room.Delete
import androidx.room.Insert
import androidx.room.OnConflictStrategy
import androidx.room.Query
import androidx.room.Update
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow

@Dao
interface PregnancyDao {

    // Pregnancy daily logs
    @Query("SELECT * FROM pregnancy_logs ORDER BY timestamp DESC")
    fun getAllPregnancyLogs(): Flow<List<PregnancyLogEntity>>

    @Query("SELECT * FROM pregnancy_logs WHERE dateString = :date LIMIT 1")
    suspend fun getLogByDate(date: String): PregnancyLogEntity?

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertOrUpdatePregnancyLog(log: PregnancyLogEntity): Long

    // Kick counter sessions
    @Query("SELECT * FROM kick_sessions ORDER BY timestamp DESC")
    fun getAllKickSessions(): Flow<List<KickSessionEntity>>

    @Insert
    suspend fun insertKickSession(session: KickSessionEntity): Long

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteKickSession(session: KickSessionEntity)

    // Baby postpartum logs
    @Query("SELECT * FROM baby_logs ORDER BY timestamp DESC")
    fun getAllBabyLogs(): Flow<List<BabyLogEntity>>

    @Insert
    suspend fun insertBabyLog(log: BabyLogEntity): Long

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteBabyLog(log: BabyLogEntity)

    // Bump Journal entries
    @Query("SELECT * FROM journal_entries ORDER BY weekNumber ASC, timestamp DESC")
    fun getAllJournalEntries(): Flow<List<JournalEntryEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertJournalEntry(entry: JournalEntryEntity): Long

    @Update
    suspend fun updateJournalEntry(entry: JournalEntryEntity)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteJournalEntry(entry: JournalEntryEntity)

    // Appointments
    @Query("SELECT * FROM appointments ORDER BY dateTimestamp ASC")
    fun getAllAppointments(): Flow<List<AppointmentEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertAppointment(appointment: AppointmentEntity): Long

    @Update
    suspend fun updateAppointment(appointment: AppointmentEntity)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteAppointment(appointment: AppointmentEntity)

    // Checklist items
    @Query("SELECT * FROM checklist_items ORDER BY id ASC")
    fun getAllChecklistItems(): Flow<List<ChecklistItemEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertChecklistItem(item: ChecklistItemEntity): Long

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertChecklistItems(items: List<ChecklistItemEntity>)

    @Update
    suspend fun updateChecklistItem(item: ChecklistItemEntity)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteChecklistItem(item: ChecklistItemEntity)

    @Query("SELECT COUNT(*) FROM checklist_items")
    suspend fun getChecklistCount(): Int

    // Medical Contacts (Doctor, Pharmacy, Clinic, etc.)
    @Query("SELECT * FROM medical_contacts ORDER BY name ASC")
    fun getAllMedicalContacts(): Flow<List<MedicalContactEntity>>

    @Insert(onConflict = OnConflictStrategy.REPLACE)
    suspend fun insertMedicalContact(contact: MedicalContactEntity): Long

    @Update
    suspend fun updateMedicalContact(contact: MedicalContactEntity)

    @Delete
    suspend fun deleteMedicalContact(contact: MedicalContactEntity)
}

package com.example.data.model

enum class SizeTheme(val displayName: String) {
    FRUITS("Fruits & Veggies"),
    ANIMALS("Cute Animals"),
    SNACKS("Snacks & Treats"),
    SPORTS("Sports Equipment")
}

data class SizeComparison(
    val title: String,
    val emoji: String,
    val description: String = ""
)

data class PregnancyWeek(
    val weekNumber: Int,
    val fruitAnalogy: String,
    val fruitEmoji: String,
    val animalAnalogy: String,
    val animalEmoji: String,
    val snackAnalogy: String,
    val snackEmoji: String,
    val sportAnalogy: String,
    val sportEmoji: String,
    val lengthCm: Double,
    val weightGrams: Double,
    val babyDevelopment: String,
    val momChanges: String,
    val clinicalAdvice: String,
    val twinAdvice: String,
    val trimester: Int,
    val commonSymptoms: List<String>
) {
    fun getComparison(theme: SizeTheme): SizeComparison {
        return when (theme) {
            SizeTheme.FRUITS -> SizeComparison(fruitAnalogy, fruitEmoji)
            SizeTheme.ANIMALS -> SizeComparison(animalAnalogy, animalEmoji)
            SizeTheme.SNACKS -> SizeComparison(snackAnalogy, snackEmoji)
            SizeTheme.SPORTS -> SizeComparison(sportAnalogy, sportEmoji)
        }
    }
}

data class ChecklistItem(
    val id: Long = 0,
    val category: String,
    val title: String,
    val isChecked: Boolean = false,
    val isCustom: Boolean = false
)

data class PrenatalAgendaItem(
    val id: Long = 0,
    val title: String,
    val weekRange: String,
    val targetWeek: Int,
    val description: String,
    val isCrucial: Boolean,
    val isCompleted: Boolean = false
)

data class BabyMilestone(
    val id: String,
    val monthRange: String,
    val targetMonth: Int,
    val domain: String, // Physical, Cognitive, Social
    val title: String,
    val description: String,
    var isAchieved: Boolean = false
)

data class KnowledgeArticle(
    val id: String,
    val category: String, // 1st Trimester, 2nd Trimester, 3rd Trimester, Postpartum, Vaccines
    val title: String,
    val readTime: String,
    val content: String
)

FILE 15/77: app/src/main/java/com/example/health/HealthConnectManager.kt

package com.example.health

import android.content.Context
import androidx.health.connect.client.HealthConnectClient
import androidx.health.connect.client.permission.HealthPermission
import androidx.health.connect.client.records.HydrationRecord
import androidx.health.connect.client.records.SleepSessionRecord
import androidx.health.connect.client.records.WeightRecord
import androidx.health.connect.client.records.metadata.Metadata
import androidx.health.connect.client.request.ReadRecordsRequest
import androidx.health.connect.client.time.TimeRangeFilter
import androidx.health.connect.client.units.Mass
import androidx.health.connect.client.units.Volume
import java.time.Instant
import java.time.ZoneOffset
import java.time.temporal.ChronoUnit
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext

enum class HealthConnectAvailability {
    INSTALLED,
    NOT_INSTALLED,
    NOT_SUPPORTED
}

data class WeightDataPoint(
    val weightKg: Double,
    val time: Instant
)

data class SleepDataPoint(
    val title: String,
    val startTime: Instant,
    val endTime: Instant,
    val durationMinutes: Long
)

class HealthConnectManager(private val context: Context) {

    private val healthConnectClient: HealthConnectClient? by lazy {
        try {
            if (HealthConnectClient.getSdkStatus(context) == HealthConnectClient.SDK_AVAILABLE) {
                HealthConnectClient.getOrCreate(context)
            } else {
                null
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            null
        }
    }

    val permissions: Set<String> = setOf(
        HealthPermission.getReadPermission(WeightRecord::class),
        HealthPermission.getWritePermission(WeightRecord::class),
        HealthPermission.getReadPermission(SleepSessionRecord::class),
        HealthPermission.getWritePermission(SleepSessionRecord::class),
        HealthPermission.getReadPermission(HydrationRecord::class),
        HealthPermission.getWritePermission(HydrationRecord::class),
    )

    fun checkAvailability(): HealthConnectAvailability {
        return try {
            val status = HealthConnectClient.getSdkStatus(context)
            when (status) {
                HealthConnectClient.SDK_AVAILABLE -> HealthConnectAvailability.INSTALLED
                HealthConnectClient.SDK_UNAVAILABLE_PROVIDER_UPDATE_REQUIRED -> HealthConnectAvailability.NOT_INSTALLED
                else -> HealthConnectAvailability.NOT_SUPPORTED
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            HealthConnectAvailability.NOT_SUPPORTED
        }
    }

    suspend fun hasAllPermissions(): Boolean = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val client = healthConnectClient ?: return@withContext false
        try {
            val granted = client.permissionController.getGrantedPermissions()
            permissions.all { it in granted }
        } catch (_: Exception) {
            false
        }
    }

    suspend fun writeWeight(weightKg: Double, time: Instant = Instant.now()): Result<Unit> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val client = healthConnectClient ?: return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Health Connect is unavailable"))
        try {
            val zoneOffset = ZoneOffset.systemDefault().rules.getOffset(time)
            val record = WeightRecord(
                weight = Mass.kilograms(weightKg),
                time = time,
                zoneOffset = zoneOffset,
                metadata = Metadata()
            )
            client.insertRecords(listOf(record))
            Result.success(Unit)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun readRecentWeights(days: Long = 30): Result<List<WeightDataPoint>> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val client = healthConnectClient ?: return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Health Connect is unavailable"))
        try {
            val startTime = Instant.now().minus(days, ChronoUnit.DAYS)
            val endTime = Instant.now()
            val response = client.readRecords(
                ReadRecordsRequest(
                    recordType = WeightRecord::class,
                    timeRangeFilter = TimeRangeFilter.between(startTime, endTime)
                )
            )
            val points = response.records.map { record ->
                WeightDataPoint(
                    weightKg = record.weight.inKilograms,
                    time = record.time
                )
            }.sortedByDescending { it.time }
            Result.success(points)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun writeSleepSession(
        startTime: Instant,
        endTime: Instant,
        title: String = "Sleep Session"
    ): Result<Unit> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val client = healthConnectClient ?: return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Health Connect is unavailable"))
        try {
            val startOffset = ZoneOffset.systemDefault().rules.getOffset(startTime)
            val endOffset = ZoneOffset.systemDefault().rules.getOffset(endTime)
            val record = SleepSessionRecord(
                startTime = startTime,
                startZoneOffset = startOffset,
                endTime = endTime,
                endZoneOffset = endOffset,
                title = title,
                metadata = Metadata()
            )
            client.insertRecords(listOf(record))
            Result.success(Unit)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun readRecentSleepSessions(days: Long = 7): Result<List<SleepDataPoint>> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val client = healthConnectClient ?: return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Health Connect is unavailable"))
        try {
            val startTime = Instant.now().minus(days, ChronoUnit.DAYS)
            val endTime = Instant.now()
            val response = client.readRecords(
                ReadRecordsRequest(
                    recordType = SleepSessionRecord::class,
                    timeRangeFilter = TimeRangeFilter.between(startTime, endTime)
                )
            )
            val points = response.records.map { record ->
                val duration = ChronoUnit.MINUTES.between(record.startTime, record.endTime)
                SleepDataPoint(
                    title = record.title ?: "Sleep Session",
                    startTime = record.startTime,
                    endTime = record.endTime,
                    durationMinutes = duration
                )
            }.sortedByDescending { it.startTime }
            Result.success(points)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }

    suspend fun writeHydration(volumeLiters: Double, time: Instant = Instant.now()): Result<Unit> = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        val client = healthConnectClient ?: return@withContext Result.failure(IllegalStateException("Health Connect is unavailable"))
        try {
            val zoneOffset = ZoneOffset.systemDefault().rules.getOffset(time)
            val record = HydrationRecord(
                startTime = time.minusSeconds(60),
                startZoneOffset = zoneOffset,
                endTime = time,
                endZoneOffset = zoneOffset,
                volume = Volume.liters(volumeLiters),
                metadata = Metadata()
            )
            client.insertRecords(listOf(record))
            Result.success(Unit)
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Result.failure(e)
        }
    }
}

package com.example.network

data class NetworkContactPayload(
    val type: String,
    val name: String,
    val phone: String?,
    val address: String? = null
)

data class RemoteContactResponse(
    val id: String,
    val name: String,
    val phone: String?
)

data class CareDirectoryResponse(
    val doctors: List<RemoteContactResponse> = emptyList(),
    val pharmacies: List<RemoteContactResponse> = emptyList(),
    val clinics: List<RemoteContactResponse> = emptyList()
)

interface MamanNetworkService {
    suspend fun getFullCareDirectory(): CareDirectoryResponse
    suspend fun uploadNewContact(payload: NetworkContactPayload): RemoteContactResponse
}

class MockMamanNetworkService : MamanNetworkService {
    override suspend fun getFullCareDirectory(): CareDirectoryResponse {
        return CareDirectoryResponse(
            doctors = listOf(RemoteContactResponse("doc_1", "Dr. Sarah Jenkins", "555-0192")),
            pharmacies = listOf(RemoteContactResponse("phar_1", "City Center Pharmacy", "555-0183")),
            clinics = listOf(RemoteContactResponse("clin_1", "Maman & Bebe Care Clinic", "555-0174"))
        )
    }

    override suspend fun uploadNewContact(payload: NetworkContactPayload): RemoteContactResponse {
        return RemoteContactResponse(
            id = "rem_${System.currentTimeMillis()}",
            name = payload.name,
            phone = payload.phone
        )
    }
}

package com.example.repository

import com.example.data.CachedContact
import com.example.data.ContactDao
import com.example.network.MamanNetworkService
import com.example.network.NetworkContactPayload
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.Flow
import java.io.IOException

class CareNetworkRepository(
    private val contactDao: ContactDao,
    private val networkService: MamanNetworkService
) {
    // Exposes real-time streams straight from the local Room database cache
    fun getCachedContacts(type: String): Flow<List<CachedContact>> = 
        contactDao.getContactsByType(type)

    fun getAllCachedContacts(): Flow<List<CachedContact>> =
        contactDao.getAllCachedContacts()

    // Synchronizes remote database tables into Room local caching storage
    suspend fun refreshDirectoryCache() {
        try {
            val response = networkService.getFullCareDirectory()
            
            val networkItems = mutableListOf<CachedContact>()
            
            // Map remote payloads to storage arrays
            response.doctors.forEach { networkItems.add(CachedContact(it.id, "DOCTOR", it.name, it.phone)) }
            response.pharmacies.forEach { networkItems.add(CachedContact(it.id, "PHARMACY", it.name, it.phone)) }
            response.clinics.forEach { networkItems.add(CachedContact(it.id, "CLINIC", it.name, it.phone)) }

            if (networkItems.isNotEmpty()) {
                contactDao.insertContacts(networkItems)
            }
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            // Network loss failover: drops request execution but preserves current Room data safely
        }
    }

    // Handles new contact creations locally first, uploading to backend if connection is active
    suspend fun addNewContact(payload: NetworkContactPayload) {
        try {
            // Upload to API first
            val remoteSaved = networkService.uploadNewContact(payload)
            val cacheItem = CachedContact(remoteSaved.id, payload.type, remoteSaved.name, remoteSaved.phone)
            contactDao.insertSingleContact(cacheItem)
        } catch (e: IOException) {
            // Offline fallback: Save with a temporary ID locally so it appears in the app immediately
            val tempId = "temp_${System.currentTimeMillis()}"
            val localFallbackItem = CachedContact(tempId, payload.type, payload.name, payload.phone)
            contactDao.insertSingleContact(localFallbackItem)
        }
    }
}

package com.example.repository

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import com.example.data.local.AppDatabase
import kotlinx.coroutines.CoroutineScope
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.launch

object PregnancySyncManager {
    private const val TAG = "PregnancySyncManager"

    /**
     * Reconciles local Room database records for symptoms and milestones with Firestore,
     * ensuring seamless offline-first data persistence.
     */
    fun startBackgroundSync(context: Context) {
        val appContext = context.applicationContext
        CoroutineScope(Dispatchers.IO).launch {
            try {
                val db = AppDatabase.getInstance(appContext)
                val dao = db.pregnancyDao()
                val syncRepo = PregnancySyncRepository(appContext, dao)

                // 1. Pull remote records from Firestore (offline-first merge)
                try {
                    syncRepo.restoreFromCloud()
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.w(TAG, "Offline or cloud restore skipped: ${e.localizedMessage}")
                }

                // 2. Push local unsynced symptom logs to Firestore
                val localLogs = dao.getAllPregnancyLogs().first()
                for (log in localLogs) {
                    try {
                        syncRepo.backupPregnancyLog(log)
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        Log.w(TAG, "Failed to sync log ${log.dateString}: ${e.localizedMessage}")
                    }
                }

                // 3. Push local milestone / journal entries to Firestore
                val localEntries = dao.getAllJournalEntries().first()
                for (entry in localEntries) {
                    try {
                        syncRepo.backupJournalEntry(entry)
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        Log.w(TAG, "Failed to sync journal entry ${entry.title}: ${e.localizedMessage}")
                    }
                }

                Log.d(TAG, "Pregnancy sync reconciliation completed successfully.")
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.e(TAG, "Pregnancy sync reconciliation failed", e)
            }
        }
    }
}

package com.example.repository

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import com.example.R
import com.example.data.local.JournalEntryEntity
import com.example.data.local.PregnancyDao
import com.example.data.local.PregnancyLogEntity
import com.google.firebase.Firebase
import com.google.firebase.auth.auth
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FieldValue
import com.google.firebase.firestore.FirebaseFirestore
import kotlinx.coroutines.tasks.await

class PregnancySyncRepository(
    private val context: Context,
    private val pregnancyDao: PregnancyDao,
    private val firestore: FirebaseFirestore
) {
    constructor(context: Context, pregnancyDao: PregnancyDao) : this(
        context = context,
        pregnancyDao = pregnancyDao,
        firestore = FirebaseFirestore.getInstance(
            context.applicationContext.getString(R.string.firestore_database_id)
        )
    )

    private val auth = Firebase.auth

    private fun requireUserId(): String {
        return auth.currentUser?.uid
            ?: throw IllegalStateException("User must be authenticated with Google to sync pregnancy data.")
    }

    /**
     * Uploads local pregnancy symptom log to Firestore for cloud backup.
     */
    suspend fun backupPregnancyLog(log: PregnancyLogEntity) {
        try {
            val uid = requireUserId()
            val logData = mapOf(
                "id" to log.id.toString(),
                "dateString" to log.dateString,
                "timestamp" to log.timestamp,
                "weightKg" to log.weightKg,
                "waterGlasses" to log.waterGlasses,
                "mood" to log.mood,
                "symptoms" to log.symptoms,
                "bloodPressure" to log.bloodPressure,
                "notes" to log.notes,
                "userId" to uid,
                "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
            )

            firestore.collection("users")
                .document(uid)
                .collection("pregnancyLogs")
                .document(log.dateString)
                .set(logData)
                .await()

            Log.d("PregnancySync", "Successfully backed up symptom log for ${log.dateString}")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e("PregnancySync", "Failed to backup pregnancy log", e)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Uploads local journal/milestone entry to Firestore for cloud backup.
     */
    suspend fun backupJournalEntry(entry: JournalEntryEntity) {
        try {
            val uid = requireUserId()
            val entryData = mapOf(
                "id" to entry.id.toString(),
                "weekNumber" to entry.weekNumber,
                "dateString" to entry.dateString,
                "timestamp" to entry.timestamp,
                "title" to entry.title,
                "reflection" to entry.reflection,
                "photoUri" to (entry.photoUri ?: ""),
                "category" to entry.category,
                "userId" to uid,
                "updatedAt" to FieldValue.serverTimestamp()
            )

            firestore.collection("users")
                .document(uid)
                .collection("journalEntries")
                .document(entry.id.toString())
                .set(entryData)
                .await()

            Log.d("PregnancySync", "Successfully backed up journal entry: ${entry.title}")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e("PregnancySync", "Failed to backup journal entry", e)
        }
    }

    /**
     * Pulls remote backups from Firestore and restores/merges them into local Room database.
     */
    suspend fun restoreFromCloud() {
        try {
            val uid = requireUserId()

            // Restore Pregnancy Logs (Symptoms)
            val logsSnapshot = firestore.collection("users")
                .document(uid)
                .collection("pregnancyLogs")
                .get()
                .await()

            for (doc in logsSnapshot.documents) {
                val dateStr = doc.getString("dateString") ?: continue
                val weight = doc.getDouble("weightKg") ?: 0.0
                val water = (doc.getLong("waterGlasses") ?: 0L).toInt()
                val mood = doc.getString("mood") ?: "Calm"
                val symptoms = doc.getString("symptoms") ?: ""
                val bp = doc.getString("bloodPressure") ?: ""
                val notes = doc.getString("notes") ?: ""
                val timestamp = doc.getLong("timestamp") ?: System.currentTimeMillis()

                val entity = PregnancyLogEntity(
                    dateString = dateStr,
                    timestamp = timestamp,
                    weightKg = weight,
                    waterGlasses = water,
                    mood = mood,
                    symptoms = symptoms,
                    bloodPressure = bp,
                    notes = notes,
                    syncedHealthConnect = true
                )
                pregnancyDao.insertOrUpdatePregnancyLog(entity)
            }

            // Restore Journal & Milestone Entries
            val journalSnapshot = firestore.collection("users")
                .document(uid)
                .collection("journalEntries")
                .get()
                .await()

            for (doc in journalSnapshot.documents) {
                val week = (doc.getLong("weekNumber") ?: 1L).toInt()
                val dateStr = doc.getString("dateString") ?: ""
                val timestamp = doc.getLong("timestamp") ?: System.currentTimeMillis()
                val title = doc.getString("title") ?: ""
                val reflection = doc.getString("reflection") ?: ""
                val photoUri = doc.getString("photoUri").takeIf { !it.isNullOrBlank() }
                val category = doc.getString("category") ?: "Belly Bump"

                val entryEntity = JournalEntryEntity(
                    weekNumber = week,
                    dateString = dateStr,
                    timestamp = timestamp,
                    title = title,
                    reflection = reflection,
                    photoUri = photoUri,
                    category = category
                )
                pregnancyDao.insertJournalEntry(entryEntity)
            }

            Log.d("PregnancySync", "Successfully restored data from Firestore cloud backup.")
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e("PregnancySync", "Failed to restore from cloud backup", e)
        }
    }
}

FILE 20/77: app/src/main/java/com/example/repository/PregnancySyncWorker.kt

package com.example.repository

import android.content.Context
import android.util.Log
import androidx.work.Constraints
import androidx.work.CoroutineWorker
import androidx.work.ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy
import androidx.work.NetworkType
import androidx.work.PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder
import androidx.work.WorkManager
import androidx.work.WorkerParameters
import com.example.data.local.AppDatabase
import kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers
import kotlinx.coroutines.flow.first
import kotlinx.coroutines.withContext
import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit

class PregnancySyncWorker(
    appContext: Context,
    workerParams: WorkerParameters
) : CoroutineWorker(appContext, workerParams) {

    companion object {
        private const val WORK_NAME = "PregnancySyncWork"

        fun scheduleSync(context: Context) {
            val constraints = Constraints.Builder()
                .setRequiredNetworkType(NetworkType.CONNECTED)
                .build()

            val syncRequest = PeriodicWorkRequestBuilder<PregnancySyncWorker>(
                repeatInterval = 6,
                repeatIntervalTimeUnit = TimeUnit.HOURS
            )
                .setConstraints(constraints)
                .build()

            WorkManager.getInstance(context.applicationContext).enqueueUniquePeriodicWork(
                WORK_NAME,
                ExistingPeriodicWorkPolicy.KEEP,
                syncRequest
            )
        }
    }

    override suspend fun doWork(): Result = withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
        try {
            val database = AppDatabase.getInstance(applicationContext)
            val dao = database.pregnancyDao()
            val syncRepository = PregnancySyncRepository(applicationContext, dao)

            // 1. Restore/fetch latest cloud records
            try {
                syncRepository.restoreFromCloud()
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                Log.w("PregnancySyncWorker", "Cloud restore skipped or offline: ${e.localizedMessage}")
            }

            // 2. Backup local symptom logs
            val localLogs = dao.getAllPregnancyLogs().first()
            for (log in localLogs) {
                try {
                    syncRepository.backupPregnancyLog(log)
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.w("PregnancySyncWorker", "Failed to backup log ${log.dateString}: ${e.localizedMessage}")
                }
            }

            // 3. Backup local journal & milestone entries
            val localEntries = dao.getAllJournalEntries().first()
            for (entry in localEntries) {
                try {
                    syncRepository.backupJournalEntry(entry)
                } catch (e: Exception) {
                    Log.w("PregnancySyncWorker", "Failed to backup journal entry ${entry.title}: ${e.localizedMessage}")
                }
            }

            Result.success()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            Log.e("PregnancySyncWorker", "Sync worker execution failed", e)
            Result.retry()
        }
    }
}

package com.example.ui.components

import android.app.Dialog
