@Composable
fun BabyLogScreen(
    viewModel: PregnancyViewModel
) {
    val babyLogs by viewModel.babyLogs.collectAsState()
    val isNursingRunning by viewModel.isNursingTimerRunning.collectAsState()
    val nursingDuration by viewModel.nursingDurationSeconds.collectAsState()
    val nursingSide by viewModel.nursingSide.collectAsState()
    val hasHealthPerms by viewModel.hasHealthPermissions.collectAsState()

    var bottleAmountMl by remember { mutableIntStateOf(90) }
    var isFormula by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var sleepMinutesSlider by remember { mutableFloatStateOf(60f) }

    val nurseMins = nursingDuration / 60
    val nurseSecs = nursingDuration % 60
    val nursingTimerFormatted = String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%02d:%02d", nurseMins, nurseSecs)

    LazyColumn(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 16.dp, bottom = 24.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        // Postpartum Banner & Title
        item {
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(20.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SoftPinkCard)
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(16.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(72.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                    ) {
                        Image(
                            painter = painterResource(id = R.drawable.illustration_baby_peaceful),
                            contentDescription = "Newborn baby",
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            contentScale = ContentScale.Crop
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(16.dp))

                    Column {
                        Text(
                            text = "Postpartum & Baby Logger",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = RosePrimary
                            ),
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("baby_logger_title")
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "Track feedings, diaper changes, and sleep patterns smoothly.",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Section 1: Nursing Timer
        item {
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .testTag("card_nursing_timer"),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
                elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 1.dp)
            ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Spa, contentDescription = null, tint = RosePrimary)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Breastfeeding Timer",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            )
                        }

                        Text(
                            text = nursingTimerFormatted,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = if (isNursingRunning) RosePrimary else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                            ),
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("nursing_timer_display")
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        Button(
                            onClick = { viewModel.toggleNursingTimer("LEFT") },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("btn_nurse_left"),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                containerColor = if (isNursingRunning && nursingSide == "LEFT") RosePrimary else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant,
                                contentColor = if (isNursingRunning && nursingSide == "LEFT") Color.White else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                            )
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = if (isNursingRunning && nursingSide == "LEFT") Icons.Default.Pause else Icons.Default.PlayArrow,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text("Left Breast")
                        }

                        Button(
                            onClick = { viewModel.toggleNursingTimer("RIGHT") },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("btn_nurse_right"),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                                containerColor = if (isNursingRunning && nursingSide == "RIGHT") RosePrimary else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant,
                                contentColor = if (isNursingRunning && nursingSide == "RIGHT") Color.White else MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                            )
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = if (isNursingRunning && nursingSide == "RIGHT") Icons.Default.Pause else Icons.Default.PlayArrow,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text("Right Breast")
                        }
                    }

                    if (nursingDuration > 0) {
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        Button(
                            onClick = { viewModel.saveNursingSession() },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .testTag("btn_save_nursing"),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = SageTertiary)
                        ) {
                            Text("Save Nursing Session ($nursingTimerFormatted)")
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Section 2: Bottle Feeding
        item {
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .testTag("card_bottle_feeding"),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SoftPeachCard)
            ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.LocalDrink, contentDescription = null, tint = Color(0xFFD97724))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Bottle Feeding",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            )
                        }
                        Text(
                            text = "$bottleAmountMl ml (${String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%.1f", bottleAmountMl / 29.57)} oz)",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                    ) {
                        FilterChip(
                            selected = !isFormula,
                            onClick = { isFormula = false },
                            label = { Text("Breastmilk") },
                            colors = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipColors(selectedContainerColor = RoseContainer)
                        )
                        FilterChip(
                            selected = isFormula,
                            onClick = { isFormula = true },
                            label = { Text("Formula") },
                            colors = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipColors(selectedContainerColor = RoseContainer)
                        )
                    }

                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        listOf(60, 90, 120, 150, 180).forEach { ml ->
                            OutlinedButton(
                                onClick = { bottleAmountMl = ml },
                                contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 2.dp),
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            ) {
                                Text("${ml}ml", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall)
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                    Button(
                        onClick = { viewModel.logBottleFeeding(bottleAmountMl, isFormula) },
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .testTag("btn_save_bottle"),
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFFD97724))
                    ) {
                        Text("Log Bottle Feeding ($bottleAmountMl ml)")
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Section 3: Diaper Tracker
        item {
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .testTag("card_diaper_tracker"),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
                elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 1.dp)
            ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                        Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Wc, contentDescription = null, tint = RosePrimary)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "Diaper Change",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        Button(
                            onClick = { viewModel.logDiaper("Wet 💧") },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("btn_diaper_wet"),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFFE1F5FE), contentColor = Color(0xFF0288D1))
                        ) {
                            Text("Wet 💧")
                        }

                        Button(
                            onClick = { viewModel.logDiaper("Dirty 💩") },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("btn_diaper_dirty"),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = Color(0xFFFFF3E0), contentColor = Color(0xFFE65100))
                        ) {
                            Text("Dirty 💩")
                        }

                        Button(
                            onClick = { viewModel.logDiaper("Both 💧💩") },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("btn_diaper_both"),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = RoseContainer, contentColor = RosePrimary)
                        ) {
                            Text("Both")
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Section 4: Sleep Session (with Health Connect sleep sync!)
        item {
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .testTag("card_sleep_tracker"),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SoftSageCard)
            ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Bedtime, contentDescription = null, tint = SageTertiary)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Sleep & Nap Tracker",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            )
                        }

                        if (hasHealthPerms) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.HealthAndSafety,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = SageTertiary,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = "Health Connect",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                        color = SageTertiary,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                    Text(
                        text = "Duration: ${sleepMinutesSlider.toInt()} minutes (${String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%.1f", sleepMinutesSlider / 60)} hours)",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                    )

                    Slider(
                        value = sleepMinutesSlider,
                        onValueChange = { sleepMinutesSlider = it },
                        valueRange = 15f..360f,
                        steps = 22,
                        colors = SliderDefaults.colors(thumbColor = SageTertiary, activeTrackColor = SageTertiary),
                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("slider_sleep_duration")
                    )

                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                    ) {
                        Button(
                            onClick = { viewModel.logSleepSession(sleepMinutesSlider.toLong(), isBaby = true) },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("btn_log_baby_sleep"),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = SageTertiary)
                        ) {
                            Text("Log Baby Sleep")
                        }

                        Button(
                            onClick = { viewModel.logSleepSession(sleepMinutesSlider.toLong(), isBaby = false) },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .weight(1f)
                                .testTag("btn_log_mom_sleep"),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.secondary)
                        ) {
                            Text("Log Mom Sleep")
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Section 5: Recent Baby Logs List
        item {
            Text(
                text = "Today's Baby Activity Log",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
            )
        }

        if (babyLogs.isEmpty()) {
            item {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "No baby events logged yet. Tap any tool above to record nursing, bottle, or diaper!",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                        ),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(24.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        } else {
            items(babyLogs) { log ->
                val timeStr = SimpleDateFormat("hh:mm a", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(log.timestamp))
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("baby_log_item_${log.id}"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
                    elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 1.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(14.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        val icon = when (log.logType) {
                            "NURSING" -> "🤱"
                            "BOTTLE" -> "🍼"
                            "DIAPER" -> "🧷"
                            "SLEEP" -> "🌙"
                            else -> "👶"
                        }
                        Text(text = icon, fontSize = 28.sp)
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(14.dp))

                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                text = "${log.logType.lowercase().replaceFirstChar { it.uppercase() }}: ${log.detail}",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            )
                            if (log.durationSeconds > 0) {
                                val m = log.durationSeconds / 60
                                val s = log.durationSeconds % 60
                                Text(
                                    text = "Duration: ${m}m ${s}s",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
                                )
                            }
                            if (log.amountMl > 0) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "Amount: ${log.amountMl} ml",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
                                )
                            }
                        }

                        Text(
                            text = timeStr,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun ChecklistScreen(
    viewModel: PregnancyViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit
) {
    val items by viewModel.checklistItems.collectAsState()
    var showAddDialog by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var newItemTitle by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var newItemCategory by remember { mutableStateOf("Hospital Bag (Mom)") }

    val checkedCount = items.count { it.isChecked }
    val totalCount = items.size

    val momItems = items.filter { it.category == "Hospital Bag (Mom)" }
    val babyItems = items.filter { it.category == "Hospital Bag (Baby)" }
    val nurseryItems = items.filter { it.category == "Nursery & Sleep" }
    val feedingItems = items.filter { it.category.contains("Feeding") || it.category.contains("Diapering") }

    val checkedPreview = items.filter { it.isChecked }.take(3).ifEmpty {
        items.take(3)
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(WarmCanvasBackground)
    ) {
        // Top Bar (matching Screen 4 "Plan an outfit" from screenshot)
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .statusBarsPadding()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
        ) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                IconButton(
                    onClick = onBack,
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_checklist_back")
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack,
                        contentDescription = "Back",
                        tint = DeepEspressoPrimary
                    )
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Plan essentials & bag",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = TextDarkEspresso
                    )
                )
            }

            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                IconButton(
                    onClick = { showAddDialog = true },
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_add_checklist_custom")
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Add,
                        contentDescription = "Add custom item",
                        tint = DeepEspressoPrimary
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Scrollable content
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 18.dp),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 4.dp, bottom = 16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(18.dp)
        ) {
            // Top Preview Card with 3 Squircles (matching Screen 4's top preview card)
            item {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .shadow(elevation = 4.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.04f))
                        .testTag("checklist_preview_card"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SquircleItemBg)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(16.dp),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
                    ) {
                        checkedPreview.forEach { item ->
                            SquircleChecklistPreview(
                                item = item,
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Category 1: Hospital Bag (Mom)
            item {
                ChecklistCategorySection(
                    title = "Hospital Bag (Mom)",
                    items = momItems,
                    onToggle = { viewModel.toggleChecklistItem(it) }
                )
            }

            // Category 2: Hospital Bag (Baby)
            item {
                ChecklistCategorySection(
                    title = "Hospital Bag (Baby)",
                    items = babyItems,
                    onToggle = { viewModel.toggleChecklistItem(it) }
                )
            }

            // Category 3: Nursery & Sleep
            item {
                ChecklistCategorySection(
                    title = "Nursery & Sleep",
                    items = nurseryItems,
                    onToggle = { viewModel.toggleChecklistItem(it) }
                )
            }

            // Category 4: Feeding & Diapering
            item {
                ChecklistCategorySection(
                    title = "Feeding & Diapering",
                    items = feedingItems,
                    onToggle = { viewModel.toggleChecklistItem(it) }
                )
            }
        }

        // Bottom Full-width Deep Espresso Pill Button (matching Screen 4 "Save as today's outfit")
        Surface(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            color = WarmCanvasBackground,
            shadowElevation = 8.dp
        ) {
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 12.dp)
            ) {
                Button(
                    onClick = onBack,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .height(52.dp)
                        .testTag("btn_save_checklist_bottom"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                        containerColor = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                        contentColor = Color.White
                    )
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "Save essentials ($checkedCount of $totalCount packed)",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = Color.White
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if (showAddDialog) {
        AlertDialog(
            onDismissRequest = { showAddDialog = false },
            title = { Text(text = "Add Essential Item", fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = TextDarkEspresso) },
            text = {
                Column {
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = newItemTitle,
                        onValueChange = { newItemTitle = it },
                        label = { Text("Item Name") },
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth().testTag("input_new_checklist_title")
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                    Text("Category:", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium)
                    val cats = listOf("Hospital Bag (Mom)", "Hospital Bag (Baby)", "Nursery & Sleep", "Feeding & Diapering")
                    LazyRow(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)) {
                        items(cats) { cat ->
                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                    .background(if (newItemCategory == cat) DeepEspressoPrimary else SoftToffeeSecondary)
                                    .clickable { newItemCategory = cat }
                                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = cat,
                                    color = if (newItemCategory == cat) Color.White else DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                    fontSize = 11.sp,
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            },
            confirmButton = {
                Button(
                    onClick = {
                        if (newItemTitle.isNotBlank()) {
                            viewModel.addChecklistItem(newItemTitle, newItemCategory)
                            newItemTitle = ""
                            showAddDialog = false
                        }
                    },
                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = DeepEspressoPrimary)
                ) {
                    Text("Add")
                }
            },
            dismissButton = {
                TextButton(onClick = { showAddDialog = false }) {
                    Text("Cancel", color = TextWarmTaupe)
                }
            }
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SquircleChecklistPreview(
    item: ChecklistItemEntity,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Column(
        modifier = modifier,
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(76.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
                .background(CrispWhiteSurface),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Text(
                text = getEmojiForItem(item.title),
                fontSize = 32.sp
            )
        }
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
        Text(
            text = item.title,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                color = TextDarkEspresso,
                fontSize = 11.sp
            ),
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun ChecklistCategorySection(
    title: String,
    items: List<ChecklistItemEntity>,
    onToggle: (ChecklistItemEntity) -> Unit
) {
    Column {
        Text(
            text = title,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = TextDarkEspresso,
                fontSize = 14.sp
            )
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))

        LazyRow(
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
        ) {
            items(items) { item ->
                SquircleChecklistItem(
                    item = item,
                    onClick = { onToggle(item) }
                )
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun SquircleChecklistItem(
    item: ChecklistItemEntity,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Column(
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
        modifier = Modifier
            .width(82.dp)
            .clickable { onClick() }
            .testTag("checklist_item_${item.id}")
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(78.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(22.dp))
                .background(if (item.isChecked) CrispWhiteSurface else SquircleItemBg)
                .border(
                    width = if (item.isChecked) 2.dp else 0.dp,
                    color = if (item.isChecked) DeepEspressoPrimary else Color.Transparent,
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(22.dp)
                ),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Text(
                text = getEmojiForItem(item.title),
                fontSize = 34.sp
            )

            // Small circular checkmark badge in top-right corner (matching Screen 4)
            if (item.isChecked) {
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                        .padding(4.dp)
                        .size(20.dp)
                        .clip(CircleShape)
                        .background(DeepEspressoPrimary),
                    contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Check,
                        contentDescription = "Checked",
                        tint = Color.White,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(13.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
        Text(
            text = item.title,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                fontWeight = if (item.isChecked) FontWeight.Bold else FontWeight.Normal,
                color = TextDarkEspresso,
                fontSize = 11.sp
            ),
            maxLines = 1,
            overflow = TextOverflow.Ellipsis,
            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
        )
    }
}

private fun getEmojiForItem(title: String): String {
    val t = title.lowercase()
    return when {
        t.contains("id") || t.contains("insurance") || t.contains("card") -> "🪪"
        t.contains("plan") || t.contains("birth") -> "📋"
        t.contains("robe") || t.contains("gown") || t.contains("nightgown") -> "👘"
        t.contains("panties") || t.contains("underwear") -> "🩲"
        t.contains("bra") -> "👙"
        t.contains("toiletries") || t.contains("toothbrush") -> "🪥"
        t.contains("charger") || t.contains("phone") -> "🔌"
        t.contains("onesie") || t.contains("sleeper") || t.contains("outfit") -> "👶"
        t.contains("blanket") || t.contains("swaddle") -> "🧣"
        t.contains("hat") || t.contains("cap") -> "🧢"
        t.contains("socks") || t.contains("booties") -> "🧦"
        t.contains("car seat") -> "🚗"
        t.contains("diaper") || t.contains("wipes") -> "🧻"
        t.contains("crib") || t.contains("bassinet") -> "🛏️"
        t.contains("mattress") -> "🛌"
        t.contains("monitor") -> "📹"
        t.contains("noise") || t.contains("sound") -> "🔊"
        t.contains("pump") || t.contains("breast pump") -> "🍼"
        t.contains("bottle") -> "🍼"
        t.contains("pacifier") -> "🍼"
        t.contains("cream") || t.contains("balm") -> "🧴"
        else -> "✨"
    }
}

package com.example.ui.screens

import androidx.activity.compose.rememberLauncherForActivityResult

import androidx.health.connect.client.PermissionController
import com.example.health.HealthConnectAvailability
import com.example.ui.theme.RoseContainer
import com.example.ui.theme.RosePrimary
import com.example.ui.theme.SageContainer
import com.example.ui.theme.SageTertiary
import com.example.ui.theme.SoftPeachCard
import com.example.ui.theme.SoftSageCard
import com.example.ui.viewmodel.PregnancyViewModel
import java.time.ZoneId
import java.time.format.DateTimeFormatter
import java.util.Locale

@Composable
fun HealthConnectDialog(
    viewModel: PregnancyViewModel,
    onDismiss: () -> Unit
) {
    val status by viewModel.healthConnectStatus.collectAsState()
    val hasPerms by viewModel.hasHealthPermissions.collectAsState()
    val syncedWeights by viewModel.syncedWeights.collectAsState()
    val syncedSleep by viewModel.syncedSleep.collectAsState()
    val currentWeight by viewModel.todayWeightKg.collectAsState()

    // Health Connect Permission Contract Launcher
    val requestPermissionsLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = PermissionController.createRequestPermissionResultContract()
    ) { grantedPermissions ->
        val allGranted = viewModel.healthConnectManager.permissions.all { it in grantedPermissions }
        viewModel.onHealthPermissionsResult(allGranted)
    }

    val dateFormatter = DateTimeFormatter.ofPattern("MMM dd, yyyy • hh:mm a")
        .withZone(ZoneId.systemDefault())

    AlertDialog(
        onDismissRequest = onDismiss,
        title = {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                Icon(
                    imageVector = Icons.Default.HealthAndSafety,
                    contentDescription = null,
                    tint = if (hasPerms) SageTertiary else RosePrimary,
                    modifier = Modifier.size(28.dp)
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Android Health Connect",
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                )
            }
        },
        text = {
            LazyColumn(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .testTag("health_connect_dialog_content"),
                verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)
            ) {
                // Security & Privacy Guarantee
                item {
                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = if (hasPerms) SoftSageCard else SoftPeachCard)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = if (hasPerms) Icons.Default.CheckCircle else Icons.Default.Lock,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = if (hasPerms) SageTertiary else RosePrimary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Column {
                                Text(
                                    text = if (hasPerms) "Secure Connection Active" else "On-Device Encrypted Health Store",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        color = if (hasPerms) SageTertiary else RosePrimary
                                    )
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = "Maman & Bébé uses Android Health Connect to read and write pregnancy weight and sleep sessions directly on your device. Your data stays private to you.",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(lineHeight = 16.sp)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Status & Action button
                item {
                    when (status) {
                        HealthConnectAvailability.NOT_SUPPORTED -> {
                            Text(
                                text = "Health Connect is not supported on this device version. Local offline logging is fully enabled.",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error)
                            )
                        }
                        HealthConnectAvailability.NOT_INSTALLED -> {
                            Text(
                                text = "Health Connect app is not installed or requires an update.",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.error)
                            )
                        }
                        HealthConnectAvailability.INSTALLED -> {
                            if (!hasPerms) {
                                Button(
                                    onClick = {
                                        requestPermissionsLauncher.launch(viewModel.healthConnectManager.permissions)
                                    },
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .testTag("btn_grant_health_connect_permissions"),
                                    colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = RosePrimary)
                                ) {
                                    Text("Grant Health Connect Permissions")
                                }
                            } else {
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = "Status: Connected & Authorized",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                                            color = SageTertiary,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold
                                        )
                                    )
                                    OutlinedButton(
                                        onClick = { viewModel.refreshHealthConnectState() },
                                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 4.dp),
                                        modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_refresh_health_connect")
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Sync, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                        Text("Sync Now", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }

                // Quick Write Test Buttons (when connected)
                if (hasPerms) {
                    item {
                        Card(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant)
                        ) {
                            Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp)) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "Quick Sync Actions",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
                                Row(
                                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Button(
                                        onClick = { viewModel.saveTodayLog() },
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .weight(1f)
                                            .testTag("btn_sync_weight_now"),
                                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = SageTertiary),
                                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Scale, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                        Text("Write $currentWeight kg", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall)
                                    }

                                    Button(
                                        onClick = { viewModel.logSleepSession(480, isBaby = false, notes = "Maternal Night Sleep") },
                                        modifier = Modifier
                                            .weight(1f)
                                            .testTag("btn_sync_sleep_now"),
                                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.secondary),
                                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                                    ) {
                                        Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Bedtime, contentDescription = null, modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp))
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                        Text("Write 8h Sleep", style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall)
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Display Synced Weight Records from Health Connect
                    item {
                        Text(
                            text = "Recent Weights in Health Connect (${syncedWeights.size})",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        )
                    }

                    if (syncedWeights.isEmpty()) {
                        item {
                            Text(
                                text = "No weight records in Health Connect yet. Tap 'Write Weight' to sync your first record.",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
                            )
                        }
                    } else {
                        items(syncedWeights.take(5)) { pt ->
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "⚖️ ${String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%.1f kg", pt.weightKg)}",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = dateFormatter.format(pt.time),
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Display Synced Sleep Sessions from Health Connect
                    item {
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "Recent Sleep Sessions in Health Connect (${syncedSleep.size})",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        )
                    }

                    if (syncedSleep.isEmpty()) {
                        item {
                            Text(
                                text = "No sleep records in Health Connect yet. Tap 'Write 8h Sleep' to record a session.",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
                            )
                        }
                    } else {
                        items(syncedSleep.take(5)) { s ->
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .fillMaxWidth()
                                    .padding(vertical = 4.dp),
                                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "🌙 ${s.title} (${s.durationMinutes} min)",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold)
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = dateFormatter.format(s.startTime),
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        },
        confirmButton = {
            Button(
                onClick = onDismiss,
                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = RosePrimary),
                modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_close_health_dialog")
            ) {
                Text("Done")
            }
        }
    )
}

package com.example.ui.screens

@Composable
fun KickCounterScreen(
    viewModel: PregnancyViewModel
) {
    val count by viewModel.kickCount.collectAsState()
    val timerSeconds by viewModel.kickTimerSeconds.collectAsState()
    val isRunning by viewModel.isKickTimerRunning.collectAsState()
    val kickSessions by viewModel.kickSessions.collectAsState()

    var sessionNotes by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
    var buttonPressed by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val scale by animateFloatAsState(
        targetValue = if (buttonPressed) 0.94f else 1f,
        label = "kickButtonScale"
    )

    val progress = (count / 10f).coerceIn(0f, 1f)
    val minutes = timerSeconds / 60
    val seconds = timerSeconds % 60
    val timeFormatted = String.format(Locale.getDefault(), "%02d:%02d", minutes, seconds)

    LazyColumn(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 16.dp, bottom = 24.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
    ) {
        // Title & Description
        item {
            Column {
                Text(
                    text = "Fetal Kick Counter",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineMedium.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = RosePrimary
                    ),
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("kick_counter_title")
                )
                Text(
                    text = "Target: 10 movements within 2 hours (ACOG standard)",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant
                    )
                )
            }
        }

        // Active Kick Session Main Card
        item {
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .testTag("kick_session_card"),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
                elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 2.dp)
            ) {
                Column(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .padding(20.dp),
                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                ) {
                    // Timer & Controls Row
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "⏱️ $timeFormatted",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface
                            ),
                            modifier = Modifier.testTag("kick_timer_display")
                        )

                        Row {
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = {
                                    if (isRunning) viewModel.pauseKickTimer() else viewModel.startKickTimer()
                                },
                                modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_kick_timer_toggle")
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = if (isRunning) Icons.Default.Pause else Icons.Default.PlayArrow,
                                    contentDescription = "Toggle Timer",
                                    tint = RosePrimary
                                )
                            }
                            IconButton(
                                onClick = { viewModel.resetKickCounter() },
                                modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_kick_reset")
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Refresh,
                                    contentDescription = "Reset Kick Counter",
                                    tint = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                    // Circular Kick Gauge & Tactile Tap Button
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier.size(220.dp),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        CircularProgressIndicator(
                            progress = { progress },
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                            strokeWidth = 12.dp,
                            color = if (count >= 10) SageTertiary else RosePrimary,
                            trackColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant
                        )

                        // Big interactive Kick Tap Button with ripple
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(175.dp)
                                .scale(scale)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(
                                    if (count >= 10) SageContainer else RoseContainer
                                )
                                .clickable(
                                    interactionSource = remember { MutableInteractionSource() },
                                    indication = ripple(bounded = true, radius = 90.dp)
                                ) {
                                    viewModel.registerKick()
                                }
                                .testTag("btn_tap_kick"),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Column(horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.TouchApp,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = if (count >= 10) SageTertiary else RosePrimary,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(36.dp)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = "$count",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.displayMedium.copy(
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        color = if (count >= 10) SageTertiary else RosePrimary
                                    )
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = if (count >= 10) "Goal Reached!" else "TAP ON KICK",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        letterSpacing = 1.sp,
                                        color = if (count >= 10) SageTertiary else RosePrimary
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                    // Celebration banner when 10 kicks reached
                    AnimatedVisibility(visible = count >= 10) {
                        Card(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .padding(vertical = 8.dp),
                            colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SoftSageCard),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
                        ) {
                            Row(
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp),
                                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.Check,
                                    contentDescription = null,
                                    tint = SageTertiary
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = "Wonderful! Baby reached 10 kicks in $timeFormatted. Great reassurance!",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                        color = SageTertiary,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    // Optional Notes & Save Button
                    OutlinedTextField(
                        value = sessionNotes,
                        onValueChange = { sessionNotes = it },
                        label = { Text("Session notes (e.g. after lunch, active rolls)") },
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .testTag("input_kick_notes"),
                        maxLines = 2
                    )

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                    Button(
                        onClick = {
                            viewModel.saveKickSession(sessionNotes)
                            sessionNotes = ""
                        },
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .testTag("btn_save_kick_session"),
                        colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
                            containerColor = if (count >= 10) SageTertiary else RosePrimary
                        ),
                        enabled = count > 0
                    ) {
                        Text("Save Kick Session Log")
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Clinical Guideline Information Card
        item {
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SoftPinkCard)
            ) {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.Top
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Info,
                        contentDescription = null,
                        tint = RosePrimary,
                        modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            text = "When to do kick counts?",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = "Starting around Week 28, count daily when baby is typically active (often in the evening or after eating). Lie on your left side to maximize blood flow. If you do not feel 10 movements in 2 hours, contact your care provider.",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(lineHeight = 18.sp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Past Sessions Log Section
        item {
            Text(
                text = "Recent Kick Sessions",
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                modifier = Modifier.padding(top = 8.dp)
            )
        }

        if (kickSessions.isEmpty()) {
            item {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surfaceVariant)
                ) {
                    Text(
                        text = "No saved kick sessions yet. Tap the button above to begin counting baby's first session!",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                            color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant,
                            textAlign = TextAlign.Center
                        ),
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(24.dp)
                    )
                }
            }
        } else {
            items(kickSessions) { session ->
                val dateStr = SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy • hh:mm a", Locale.getDefault())
                    .format(Date(session.timestamp))
                val sessMinutes = session.durationSeconds / 60
                val sessSecs = session.durationSeconds % 60

                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .testTag("kick_session_item_${session.id}"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.surface),
                    elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 1.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(14.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(44.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(if (session.targetReached) SageContainer else RoseContainer),
                            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "${session.kickCount}",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = if (session.targetReached) SageTertiary else RosePrimary
                                )
                            )
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))

                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Text(
                                text = if (session.targetReached) "Goal Reached (10 kicks)" else "${session.kickCount} kicks recorded",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "Duration: ${sessMinutes}m ${sessSecs}s • $dateStr",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurfaceVariant)
                            )
                            if (session.notes.isNotBlank()) {
                                Text(
                                    text = "Notes: ${session.notes}",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                        color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.onSurface,
                                        fontStyle = androidx.compose.ui.text.font.FontStyle.Italic
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
fun KnowledgeScreen(
    viewModel: PregnancyViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit
) {
    val articles = MilestoneRoadmapData.knowledgeArticles
    var selectedCategory by remember { mutableStateOf("All") }
    val categories = listOf("All", "Vaccines", "1st Trimester", "3rd Trimester", "Postpartum")

    val filteredList = if (selectedCategory == "All") {
        articles
    } else {
        articles.filter { it.category == selectedCategory }
    }

    LazyColumn(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
        contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 16.dp, bottom = 32.dp),
        verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
    ) {
        // Header
        item {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                IconButton(onClick = onBack, modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_knowledge_back")) {
                    Icon(imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack, contentDescription = "Back", tint = SoftRose)
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                Column {
                    Text(
                        text = "Clinical Knowledge Base",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = SoftRose)
                    )
                    Text(
                        text = "Evidence-based guides, vaccine agendas, and labor preparation",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Category Filter Chips
        item {
            LazyRow(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)) {
                items(categories) { cat ->
                    FilterChip(
                        selected = selectedCategory == cat,
                        onClick = { selectedCategory = cat },
                        label = { Text(cat, style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall) },
                        colors = FilterChipDefaults.filterChipColors(
                            selectedContainerColor = SoftRose,
                            selectedLabelColor = Color.White
                        )
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        items(filteredList) { article ->
            val isVaccine = article.category == "Vaccines"
            Card(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .testTag("knowledge_article_${article.id}"),
                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                    containerColor = if (isVaccine) SageGreenContainer else SoftPeachCard
                ),
                elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 1.dp)
            ) {
                Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = article.category,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = if (isVaccine) SageGreen else SoftRose
                            )
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = article.readTime,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(color = MaterialTheme.colorScheme.outline)
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))

                    Text(
                        text = article.title,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold)
                    )

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                    Text(
                        text = article.content,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(lineHeight = 22.sp)
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

enum class AssistantColorTheme(

    val emoji: String,
    val primary: Color,
    val onPrimary: Color,

    val headerEnd: Color,

    val badgeBg: Color
) {
    ROSE(
        title = "Rose Blush",
        emoji = "🌸",
        primary = Color(0xFFE26D77),
        onPrimary = Color.White,
        headerStart = Color(0xFFFFECEF),
        headerEnd = Color(0xFFFDE0E4),
        canvasBg = Color(0xFFFFF9FA),
        bubbleUser = Color(0xFFE26D77),
        bubbleAi = Color.White,
        border = Color(0xFFF5CCD2),
        badgeBg = Color(0xFFFFEEF0)
    ),
    SAGE(
        title = "Gentle Sage",
        emoji = "🌿",
        primary = Color(0xFF4D7C66),
        onPrimary = Color.White,
        headerStart = Color(0xFFEAF5EE),
        headerEnd = Color(0xFFDCEDE2),
        canvasBg = Color(0xFFF7FAF8),
        bubbleUser = Color(0xFF4D7C66),
        bubbleAi = Color.White,
        border = Color(0xFFC7E2D3),
        badgeBg = Color(0xFFEDF7F1)
    ),
    LAVENDER(
        title = "Soft Lavender",
        emoji = "💜",

        headerStart = Color(0xFFF3EDFA),
        headerEnd = Color(0xFFE8DEF5),
