import com.example.ui.viewmodel.PregnancyViewModel

@Composable
fun StatsScreen(
    viewModel: PregnancyViewModel,
    onOpenHealthConnectDialog: () -> Unit
) {
    val currentWeek by viewModel.currentWeekNumber.collectAsState()
    val hasHealthPerms by viewModel.hasHealthPermissions.collectAsState()
    val todayWeight by viewModel.todayWeightKg.collectAsState()
    val waterGlasses by viewModel.todayWaterGlasses.collectAsState()
    val logs by viewModel.pregnancyLogs.collectAsState()
    val kickSessions by viewModel.kickSessions.collectAsState()
    val babyLogs by viewModel.babyLogs.collectAsState()
    val syncedWeights by viewModel.syncedWeights.collectAsState()
    val syncedSleep by viewModel.syncedSleep.collectAsState()

    val totalLogs = logs.size + kickSessions.size + babyLogs.size + syncedWeights.size
    val totalKicks = kickSessions.sumOf { it.kickCount }
    val avgSleepHours = if (syncedSleep.isNotEmpty()) {
        syncedSleep.map { it.durationMinutes / 60.0 }.average()
    } else {
        7.5
    }

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(WarmCanvasBackground)
    ) {
        // Top Peach Header (matching Screen 3 "Stats" from screenshot)
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .background(PeachHeaderBackground)
                .statusBarsPadding()
                .padding(horizontal = 20.dp, vertical = 18.dp)
        ) {
            Column {
                Text(
                    text = "YOUR PREGNANCY IN NUMBERS",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                        letterSpacing = 1.2.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        color = TextWarmTaupe,
                        fontSize = 10.sp
                    )
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Stats",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineLarge.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = TextDarkEspresso,
                        fontSize = 32.sp
                    )
                )
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))

                // 2 Pill Badges (matching [14 items] [3 outfits saved] from Screen 3)
                Row(
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            .background(CrispWhiteSurface.copy(alpha = 0.85f))
                            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp)
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.CalendarMonth,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(13.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "Week $currentWeek",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                    color = TextDarkEspresso,
                                    fontSize = 11.sp
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }

                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                            .background(CrispWhiteSurface.copy(alpha = 0.85f))
                            .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 5.dp)
                    ) {
                        Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.CheckCircle,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(13.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = "$totalLogs logs saved",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                    color = TextDarkEspresso,
                                    fontSize = 11.sp
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        // Scrollable content
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(horizontal = 18.dp),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 16.dp, bottom = 90.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            // Card 1: Health & Body Metrics (matching "Wardrobe colors" card in Screen 3)
            item {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .shadow(elevation = 6.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.05f))
                        .testTag("stats_health_card"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = CrispWhiteSurface)
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp)) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Health & Body metrics",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = TextDarkEspresso
                            )
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
                        Row(
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.Bottom,
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(6.dp)
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = "5 key metrics",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleLarge.copy(
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = DeepEspressoPrimary
                                )
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = if (hasHealthPerms) "synced with Health Connect" else "ready to sync",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                    color = TextWarmTaupe,
                                    fontSize = 11.sp
                                ),
                                modifier = Modifier.padding(bottom = 2.dp)
                            )
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))

                        // Segmented color bar (matching the multi-color bar in Screen 3)
                        val colorEspresso = Color(0xFF4E260D)
                        val colorCaramel = Color(0xFFC48B5E)
                        val colorDenim = Color(0xFF5A758E)
                        val colorTaupe = Color(0xFF8C7A6B)
                        val colorPeach = Color(0xFFE5B896)

                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(20.dp)
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                        ) {
                            Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(0.30f).fillMaxSize().background(colorEspresso))
                            Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(0.24f).fillMaxSize().background(colorCaramel))
                            Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(0.18f).fillMaxSize().background(colorDenim))
                            Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(0.15f).fillMaxSize().background(colorTaupe))
                            Box(modifier = Modifier.weight(0.13f).fillMaxSize().background(colorPeach))
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(18.dp))

                        // Breakdown items with dot, label, mini progress bar, value
                        MetricBreakdownRow(
                            dotColor = colorEspresso,
                            label = "Weight",
                            progress = 0.85f,
                            value = "${todayWeight} kg"
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        MetricBreakdownRow(
                            dotColor = colorCaramel,
                            label = "Sleep Avg",
                            progress = 0.72f,
                            value = String.format("%.1f hrs", avgSleepHours)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        MetricBreakdownRow(
                            dotColor = colorDenim,
                            label = "Hydration",
                            progress = (waterGlasses / 8f).coerceIn(0f, 1f),
                            value = "${waterGlasses * 250} ml"
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        MetricBreakdownRow(
                            dotColor = colorTaupe,
                            label = "Fetal Kicks",
                            progress = 0.60f,
                            value = "$totalKicks kicks"
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                        MetricBreakdownRow(
                            dotColor = colorPeach,
                            label = "Blood Pressure",
                            progress = 0.45f,
                            value = "118 / 76"
                        )

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                        // Health Connect Sync action button
                        Button(
                            onClick = onOpenHealthConnectDialog,
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .fillMaxWidth()
                                .height(44.dp)
                                .testTag("btn_sync_health_connect"),
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(22.dp),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = SoftToffeeSecondary)
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = if (hasHealthPerms) Icons.Default.HealthAndSafety else Icons.Default.Sync,
                                contentDescription = null,
                                tint = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(16.dp)
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = if (hasHealthPerms) "Health Connect Synced" else "Connect Android Health",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = DeepEspressoPrimary
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Card 2: Most logged (matching "Most worn" card in Screen 3)
            item {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .shadow(elevation = 6.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.05f))
                        .testTag("stats_most_logged_card"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = CrispWhiteSurface)
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp)) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Most logged activities",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = TextDarkEspresso
                            )
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))

                        // Row 1: Kicks
                        MostLoggedRow(
                            emoji = "🦶",
                            title = "Fetal movements",
                            progress = 0.85f,
                            countText = "${kickSessions.size.coerceAtLeast(8)} sessions"
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                        // Row 2: Weight logs
                        MostLoggedRow(
                            emoji = "⚖️",
                            title = "Maternal weight",
                            progress = 0.65f,
                            countText = "${syncedWeights.size.coerceAtLeast(6)} entries"
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                        // Row 3: Water hydration
                        MostLoggedRow(
                            emoji = "💧",
                            title = "Daily hydration",
                            progress = 0.55f,
                            countText = "${waterGlasses} glasses"
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                        // Row 4: Daily symptoms
                        MostLoggedRow(
                            emoji = "📝",
                            title = "Symptom check-ins",
                            progress = 0.40f,
                            countText = "${logs.size.coerceAtLeast(4)} days"
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MetricBreakdownRow(
    dotColor: Color,
    label: String,
    progress: Float,
    value: String
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(8.dp)
                .clip(CircleShape)
                .background(dotColor)
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
        Text(
            text = label,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Medium,
                color = TextDarkEspresso,
                fontSize = 12.sp
            ),
            modifier = Modifier.width(84.dp)
        )
        LinearProgressIndicator(
            progress = { progress },
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .height(5.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)),
            color = dotColor,
            trackColor = SquircleItemBg
        )
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
        Text(
            text = value,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                color = TextDarkEspresso,
                fontSize = 12.sp
            )
        )
    }
}

@Composable
private fun MostLoggedRow(
    emoji: String,
    title: String,
    progress: Float,
    countText: String
) {
    Row(
        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
    ) {
        // Squircle thumbnail
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(44.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .background(SquircleItemBg),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Text(text = emoji, fontSize = 20.sp)
        }
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(12.dp))
        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = title,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        color = TextDarkEspresso
                    )
                )
                Text(
                    text = countText,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = TextWarmTaupe,
                        fontSize = 11.sp
                    )
                )
            }
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
            LinearProgressIndicator(
                progress = { progress },
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .height(6.dp)
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(3.dp)),
                color = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                trackColor = SquircleItemBg
            )
        }
    }
}

package com.example.ui.screens

import android.app.DatePickerDialog

import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Calendar
import java.util.Date

@Composable
fun TodayScreen(
    viewModel: PregnancyViewModel,
    onNavigateWeekDetail: () -> Unit,
    onOpenHealthConnectDialog: () -> Unit,
    onOpenLanguageDialog: () -> Unit = {},
    onOpenSettings: () -> Unit = {}
) {
    val currentWeek by viewModel.currentWeekNumber.collectAsState()
    val weekData by viewModel.currentWeekData.collectAsState()
    val dueDateMillis by viewModel.dueDateMillis.collectAsState()
    val isTwinMode by viewModel.isTwinMode.collectAsState()
    val sizeTheme by viewModel.selectedSizeTheme.collectAsState()
    val calendarDays by viewModel.calendarStrip.collectAsState()
    val selectedDate by viewModel.selectedCalendarDate.collectAsState()
    val hasHealthPerms by viewModel.hasHealthPermissions.collectAsState()
    val todayWeight by viewModel.todayWeightKg.collectAsState()
    val healthMsg by viewModel.healthConnectMessage.collectAsState()
    val currentLanguage by viewModel.currentLanguage.collectAsState()
    val kickSessions by viewModel.kickSessions.collectAsState()
    val appointments by viewModel.appointments.collectAsState()
    val journalEntries by viewModel.journalEntries.collectAsState()
    val context = LocalContext.current

    val fullTodayDate = SimpleDateFormat("EEEE, MMMM d", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date()).uppercase()
    val dueDateFormatted = SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(dueDateMillis))
    val diffDays = ((dueDateMillis - System.currentTimeMillis()) / (1000L * 3600 * 24)).toInt().coerceAtLeast(0)

    val currentComparison = weekData.getComparison(sizeTheme)

    val flagEmoji = when (currentLanguage) {
        "fr" -> "🇫🇷 FR"
        "es" -> "🇪🇸 ES"
        "pt" -> "🇵🇹 PT"
        "it" -> "🇮🇹 IT"
        "ar" -> "🇸🇦 AR"
        else -> "🇺🇸 EN"
    }

    // Photo Activity Launchers (Standard shortcuts for Photo Camera & Gallery access)
    var photoFeedbackMessage by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }
    var selectedAlbumForDialog by remember { mutableStateOf<com.example.ui.components.HomeAlbumDef?>(null) }

    val cameraLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.TakePicturePreview()
    ) { bitmap ->
        if (bitmap != null) {
            viewModel.saveBumpPhoto(
                bitmap = bitmap,
                week = currentWeek,
                title = "Week $currentWeek Bump Photo",
                notes = "Captured with camera on $fullTodayDate"
            )
            photoFeedbackMessage = "Week $currentWeek bump photo captured and saved!"
            Toast.makeText(context, "Bump photo saved to your journal!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    val galleryLauncher = rememberLauncherForActivityResult(
        contract = ActivityResultContracts.PickVisualMedia()
    ) { uri ->
        if (uri != null) {
            viewModel.saveBumpPhotoUri(
                uriString = uri.toString(),
                week = currentWeek,
                title = "Week $currentWeek Bump Photo",
                notes = "Imported from gallery"
            )
            photoFeedbackMessage = "Photo imported from gallery and saved!"
            Toast.makeText(context, "Photo added to your journal!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    // Sort upcoming appointments
    val upcomingAppointments = remember(appointments) {
        appointments.filter { !it.isCompleted }.sortedBy { it.dateTimestamp }
    }
    val nextAppointment = upcomingAppointments.firstOrNull()

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(WarmCanvasBackground)
            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp)
    ) {
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
        // Compact Greeting & Date Row
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
        ) {
            Column {
                Text(
                    text = fullTodayDate,
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                        letterSpacing = 0.8.sp,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                        color = TextWarmTaupe,
                        fontSize = 9.sp
                    )
                )
                Text(
                    text = stringResource(id = R.string.good_morning),
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineSmall.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = TextDarkEspresso,
                        fontSize = 20.sp
                    )
                )
            }

            // Week summary pill in header
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                    .background(DeepEspressoPrimary)
                    .clickable { onNavigateWeekDetail() }
                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
            ) {
                Text(
                    text = "Week $currentWeek",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                        color = Color.White,
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        fontSize = 11.sp
                    )
                )
            }
        }
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

        // Main Scrollable Dashboard
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxSize()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 12.dp, bottom = 90.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(14.dp)
        ) {


            // Notification / Feedback banner if present
            if (photoFeedbackMessage != null || healthMsg != null) {
                item {
                    val msg = photoFeedbackMessage ?: healthMsg ?: ""
                    Card(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SoftToffeeSecondary),
                        shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp)
                    ) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.padding(12.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.CheckCircle, contentDescription = null, tint = DeepEspressoPrimary)
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                            Text(text = msg, style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium, modifier = Modifier.weight(1f))
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(id = R.string.dismiss),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold),
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clickable {
                                        photoFeedbackMessage = null
                                        viewModel.clearHealthMessage()
                                    }
                                    .padding(4.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // 1. Editable Pregnancy Countdown Banner on Top matching reference design
            item {
                com.example.ui.components.PregnancyCountdownHeroCard(
                    currentWeek = currentWeek,
                    weekData = weekData,
                    diffDays = diffDays,
                    sizeTheme = sizeTheme,
                    onEditDueDate = {
                        val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
                        cal.timeInMillis = dueDateMillis
                        DatePickerDialog(
                            context,
                            { _, year, month, day ->
                                val pickedCal = Calendar.getInstance()
                                pickedCal.set(year, month, day)
                                viewModel.updateDueDate(pickedCal.timeInMillis)
                            },
                            cal.get(Calendar.YEAR),
                            cal.get(Calendar.MONTH),
                            cal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH)
                        ).show()
                    }
                )
            }

            // 2. Large Monthly Tracker at the bottom of the Pregnancy Countdown section
            item {
                MonthlyPregnancyTracker(
                    selectedDateString = selectedDate,
                    onDateSelected = { date -> viewModel.selectCalendarDate(date) },
                    currentGestationalWeek = currentWeek
                )
            }

            // 3. Interactive Visual Model with Audio Descriptions & Size Guide
            item {
                InteractiveAudioGrowthView(
                    currentWeek = currentWeek,
                    weekData = weekData,
                    selectedTheme = sizeTheme,
                    onThemeChanged = { newTheme -> viewModel.setSizeTheme(newTheme) }
                )
            }

            // 4. Pregnancy Timeline highlighting important milestones on Home screen
            item {
                PregnancyMilestoneTimeline(
                    currentWeek = currentWeek
                )
            }

            // 4. Upcoming Doctor Appointment data directly on Home Screen
            item {
                UpcomingDoctorAppointmentsCard(
                    nextAppointment = nextAppointment,
                    totalAppointmentsCount = upcomingAppointments.size,
                    onAddAppointmentClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.AGENDA) },
                    onViewAgendaClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.AGENDA) },
                    onToggleComplete = { app -> viewModel.toggleAppointmentCompleted(app) },
                    onExportPdfClick = {
                        val exportedContacts = viewModel.cachedContacts.value.map { c ->
                            com.example.data.local.MedicalContactEntity(
                                id = 0,
                                type = c.type,
                                name = c.name,
                                phone = c.phone ?: "",
                                address = c.address ?: "",
                                notes = c.notes ?: ""
                            )
                        }
                        val file = com.example.util.CareDirectoryPdfExporter.generateCareDirectoryPdf(
                            context = context,
                            currentWeek = currentWeek,
                            dueDateMillis = dueDateMillis,
                            appointments = appointments,
                            contacts = exportedContacts
                        )
                        if (file != null) {
                            Toast.makeText(context, "Care directory PDF generated!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                            com.example.util.CareDirectoryPdfExporter.sharePdf(context, file)
                        } else {
                            Toast.makeText(context, "Failed to generate PDF", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                        }
                    }
                )
            }

            // 5. Standard Shortcuts/Buttons for Photo Camera and Gallery Access on Home Screen
            item {
                BumpPhotoStudioCard(
                    currentWeek = currentWeek,
                    savedPhotosCount = journalEntries.count { it.photoUri != null },
                    onCameraClick = { cameraLauncher.launch(null) },
                    onGalleryClick = {
                        galleryLauncher.launch(
                            PickVisualMediaRequest(ActivityResultContracts.PickVisualMedia.ImageOnly)
                        )
                    },
                    onViewJournalAlbumClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.JOURNAL) }
                )
            }

            // Photo Album Highlights Carousel (Family, Ultrasound, Belly Bump, Baby Milestones, Nursery)
            item {
                com.example.ui.components.HomePhotoAlbumsCarousel(
                    entries = journalEntries,
                    onAlbumSelected = { album ->
                        selectedAlbumForDialog = album
                    }
                )
            }

            // 6. Featured Big Stage Card (Daily Journey & Size Comparison)
            item {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .shadow(elevation = 5.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.06f))
                        .testTag("featured_stage_card"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = CrispWhiteSurface)
                ) {
                    Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp)) {
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Column {
                                Text(
                                    text = stringResource(id = R.string.todays_journey),
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                        letterSpacing = 1.sp,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        color = TextWarmTaupe,
                                        fontSize = 10.sp
                                    )
                                )
                                Text(
                                    text = if (isTwinMode) {
                                        stringResource(id = R.string.twin_week_trimester, currentWeek)
                                    } else {
                                        stringResource(id = R.string.week_trimester, currentWeek, weekData.trimester)
                                    },
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = TextDarkEspresso)
                                )
                            }

                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                                    .background(DeepEspressoPrimary)
                                    .clickable {
                                        val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
                                        cal.timeInMillis = dueDateMillis
                                        DatePickerDialog(
                                            context,
                                            { _, year, month, day ->
                                                val pickedCal = Calendar.getInstance()
                                                pickedCal.set(year, month, day)
                                                viewModel.updateDueDate(pickedCal.timeInMillis)
                                            },
                                            cal.get(Calendar.YEAR),
                                            cal.get(Calendar.MONTH),
                                            cal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH)
                                        ).show()
                                    }
                                    .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                                    .testTag("due_date_pill")
                            ) {
                                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = stringResource(id = R.string.days_left, diffDays),
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                            color = Color.White,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            fontSize = 11.sp
                                        )
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                                    Icon(
                                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Edit,
                                        contentDescription = "Edit due date",
                                        tint = Color.White,
                                        modifier = Modifier.size(12.dp)
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                        // 3 Squircle Item Thumbnails
                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                        ) {
                            SquircleThumbnail(
                                title = currentComparison.title,
                                emojiOrIcon = currentComparison.emoji,
                                subtitle = "~${weekData.lengthCm} cm",
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                onClick = onNavigateWeekDetail
                            )

                            SquircleThumbnail(
                                title = "${todayWeight} kg",
                                emojiOrIcon = "⚖️",
                                subtitle = if (hasHealthPerms) "Synced" else "Logged",
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                onClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.HEALTH_LOG) }
                            )

                            SquircleThumbnail(
                                title = "10 Kicks",
                                emojiOrIcon = "🦶",
                                subtitle = stringResource(id = R.string.daily_kick_goal),
                                modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                                onClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.KICK_COUNTER) }
                            )
                        }

                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))

                        Row(
                            modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp),
                            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                        ) {
                            Button(
                                onClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.HEALTH_LOG) },
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .weight(1f)
                                    .height(48.dp)
                                    .testTag("btn_wear_it_style_cta"),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(24.dp),
                                colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = DeepEspressoPrimary)
                            ) {
                                Text(
                                    text = stringResource(id = R.string.log_todays_symptoms),
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                                )
                            }

                            Box(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .size(48.dp)
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                    .background(SquircleItemBg)
                                    .clickable { onNavigateWeekDetail() }
                                    .testTag("btn_expand_journey"),
                                contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                            ) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.OpenInFull,
                                    contentDescription = "Expand Guide",
                                    tint = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // 7. Compact Pill Badges Row
            item {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
                ) {
                    CompactPillBadge(
                        icon = "👶",
                        text = stringResource(id = R.string.week_of_40, currentWeek),
                        onClick = onNavigateWeekDetail
                    )
                    CompactPillBadge(
                        icon = "🗓️",
                        text = stringResource(id = R.string.days_remaining, diffDays),
                        onClick = {
                            val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
                            cal.timeInMillis = dueDateMillis
                            DatePickerDialog(
                                context,
                                { _, year, month, day ->
                                    val pickedCal = Calendar.getInstance()
                                    pickedCal.set(year, month, day)
                                    viewModel.updateDueDate(pickedCal.timeInMillis)
                                },
                                cal.get(Calendar.YEAR),
                                cal.get(Calendar.MONTH),
                                cal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_MONTH)
                            ).show()
                        }
                    )
                }
            }

            // 8. Search Bar Pill
            item {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(24.dp))
                        .clickable { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.ASSISTANT) }
                        .testTag("search_bar_pill"),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = CrispWhiteSurface),
                    elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = 1.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 14.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Search,
                            contentDescription = "Search",
                            tint = TextWarmTaupe,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(id = R.string.search_placeholder),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(color = TextWarmTaupe, fontSize = 13.sp)
                        )
                    }
                }
            }

            // 9. Interactive 14-Day Calendar Strip
            item {
                Column {
                    Text(
                        text = "14-Day Daily Timeline",
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = TextDarkEspresso)
                    )
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(10.dp))
                    LazyRow(
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp),
                        contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 2.dp)
                    ) {
                        items(calendarDays) { day ->
                            val isSelected = day.dateString == selectedDate
                            Card(
                                modifier = Modifier
                                    .width(54.dp)
                                    .clip(RoundedCornerShape(16.dp))
                                    .clickable {
                                        viewModel.selectCalendarDate(day.dateString)
                                        if (day.hasLog) {
                                            viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.HEALTH_LOG)
                                        }
                                    }
                                    .testTag("calendar_day_${day.dayOfMonth}"),
                                shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
                                colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
                                    containerColor = if (isSelected) DeepEspressoPrimary else CrispWhiteSurface
                                ),
                                elevation = CardDefaults.cardElevation(defaultElevation = if (isSelected) 3.dp else 1.dp)
                            ) {
                                Column(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .fillMaxWidth()
                                        .padding(vertical = 10.dp),
                                    horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = day.dayOfWeek,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                            color = if (isSelected) Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.8f) else TextWarmTaupe,
                                            fontSize = 10.sp
                                        )
                                    )
                                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                                    Text(
                                        text = day.dayOfMonth,
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            color = if (isSelected) Color.White else TextDarkEspresso,
                                            fontSize = 15.sp
                                        )
                                    )
                                    if (day.hasLog) {
                                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(4.dp))
                                        Box(
                                            modifier = Modifier
                                                .size(5.dp)
                                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                                .background(if (isSelected) Color.White else DeepEspressoPrimary)
                                        )
                                    }
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // 10. "Recently logged" Carousel
            item {
                Column {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(id = R.string.recently_logged),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = TextDarkEspresso)
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(id = R.string.see_all),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = DeepEspressoPrimary),
                            modifier = Modifier.clickable { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.HEALTH_LOG) }
                        )
                    }

                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))

                    LazyRow(horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(12.dp)) {
                        item {
                            RecentLogCard(
                                title = "Maternal Weight",
                                subtitle = "${todayWeight} kg logged",
                                icon = "⚖️",
                                onClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.HEALTH_LOG) }
                            )
                        }
                        item {
                            RecentLogCard(
                                title = "Kick Session",
                                subtitle = "${kickSessions.size} recorded",
                                icon = "🦶",
                                onClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.KICK_COUNTER) }
                            )
                        }
                        item {
                            RecentLogCard(
                                title = "Baby Care",
                                subtitle = "Nursing & sleep logs",
                                icon = "🍼",
                                onClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.BABY_LOG) }
                            )
                        }
                        item {
                            RecentLogCard(
                                title = "AI Guidance",
                                subtitle = "Clinical Q&A",
                                icon = "✨",
                                onClick = { viewModel.selectTab(ScreenTab.ASSISTANT) }
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // 11. Medical Disclaimer Card
            item {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(18.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = CrispWhiteSurface)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.padding(14.dp),
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.Shield,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(id = R.string.medical_disclaimer),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                color = TextWarmTaupe,
                                fontSize = 11.sp,
                                lineHeight = 16.sp
                            )
                        )
                    }
                }
            }
        }

        if (selectedAlbumForDialog != null) {
            com.example.ui.components.PhotoAlbumDetailDialog(
                album = selectedAlbumForDialog!!,
                viewModel = viewModel,
                onDismiss = { selectedAlbumForDialog = null }
            )
        }
    }
}

// Doctor Appointments Card directly on Home Screen
@Composable
private fun UpcomingDoctorAppointmentsCard(
    nextAppointment: AppointmentEntity?,
    totalAppointmentsCount: Int,
    onAddAppointmentClick: () -> Unit,
    onViewAgendaClick: () -> Unit,
    onToggleComplete: (AppointmentEntity) -> Unit,
    onExportPdfClick: (() -> Unit)? = null
) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .shadow(elevation = 5.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.05f))
            .testTag("upcoming_appointments_card"),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = CrispWhiteSurface)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp)) {
            // Header Row
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(36.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(SoftToffeeSecondary),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.MedicalServices,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(id = R.string.upcoming_appointments),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = TextDarkEspresso,
                                fontSize = 15.sp
                            )
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = if (totalAppointmentsCount > 0) "$totalAppointmentsCount scheduled visits" else "Prenatal care timeline",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(color = TextWarmTaupe, fontSize = 10.sp)
                        )
                    }
                }

                Row(
                    verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                ) {
                    if (onExportPdfClick != null) {
                        Box(
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                                .background(SoftToffeeSecondary)
                                .clickable { onExportPdfClick() }
                                .padding(horizontal = 8.dp, vertical = 4.dp)
                                .testTag("btn_home_export_pdf")
                        ) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Icon(
                                    imageVector = Icons.Default.PictureAsPdf,
                                    contentDescription = "PDF Export",
                                    tint = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                    modifier = Modifier.size(12.dp)
                                )
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(3.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = "PDF",
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                        color = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                        fontSize = 11.sp
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                        }
                    }

                    Text(
                        text = stringResource(id = R.string.view_agenda),
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = DeepEspressoPrimary
                        ),
                        modifier = Modifier.clickable { onViewAgendaClick() }
                    )
                }
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))

            if (nextAppointment != null) {
                // Next scheduled appointment card
                val dateFmt = SimpleDateFormat("EEE, MMM dd • hh:mm a", Locale.getDefault())
                    .format(Date(nextAppointment.dateTimestamp))

                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                        .background(SquircleItemBg)
                        .padding(14.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Column(modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)) {
                            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                                Box(
                                    modifier = Modifier
                                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
                                        .background(DeepEspressoPrimary)
                                        .padding(horizontal = 6.dp, vertical = 2.dp)
                                ) {
                                    Text(
                                        text = "NEXT VISIT",
                                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                            color = Color.White,
                                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                            fontSize = 9.sp
                                        )
                                    )
                                }
                                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
                                Text(
                                    text = dateFmt,
                                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                        color = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                        fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                        fontSize = 11.sp
                                    )
                                )
                            }
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(6.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = nextAppointment.title,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                    color = TextDarkEspresso,
                                    fontSize = 14.sp
                                )
                            )
                            Text(
                                text = "${nextAppointment.doctorName} • ${nextAppointment.location}",
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                    color = TextWarmTaupe,
                                    fontSize = 11.sp
                                )
                            )
                        }

                        IconButton(
                            onClick = { onToggleComplete(nextAppointment) },
                            modifier = Modifier
                                .size(36.dp)
                                .clip(CircleShape)
                                .background(CrispWhiteSurface)
                        ) {
                            Icon(
                                imageVector = Icons.Default.CheckCircle,
                                contentDescription = "Complete appointment",
                                tint = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                modifier = Modifier.size(20.dp)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            } else {
                // Empty state with quick add CTA
                Box(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .clip(RoundedCornerShape(18.dp))
                        .background(SquircleItemBg)
                        .padding(14.dp)
                ) {
                    Row(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                        horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                        verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(id = R.string.no_upcoming_appointments),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.copy(
                                color = TextWarmTaupe,
                                fontSize = 12.sp
                            ),
                            modifier = Modifier.weight(1f)
                        )
                        Button(
                            onClick = onAddAppointmentClick,
                            shape = RoundedCornerShape(14.dp),
                            colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(containerColor = DeepEspressoPrimary),
                            contentPadding = PaddingValues(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                        ) {
                            Icon(imageVector = Icons.Default.Add, contentDescription = null, tint = Color.White, modifier = Modifier.size(14.dp))
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = stringResource(id = R.string.schedule_appointment),
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = Color.White)
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

// Bump & Baby Photo Studio with Camera and Gallery access shortcuts
@Composable
private fun BumpPhotoStudioCard(
    currentWeek: Int,
    savedPhotosCount: Int,
    onCameraClick: () -> Unit,
    onGalleryClick: () -> Unit,
    onViewJournalAlbumClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Card(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxWidth()
            .shadow(elevation = 5.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.05f))
            .testTag("bump_photo_studio_card"),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = CrispWhiteSurface)
    ) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(18.dp)) {
            // Header Row
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween,
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
            ) {
                Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .size(36.dp)
                            .clip(CircleShape)
                            .background(SoftToffeeSecondary),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.CameraAlt,
                            contentDescription = null,
                            tint = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                    }
                    Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(10.dp))
                    Column {
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(id = R.string.bump_photo_studio),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleSmall.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = TextDarkEspresso,
                                fontSize = 15.sp
                            )
                        )
                        Text(
                            text = "Week $currentWeek • $savedPhotosCount memories saved",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(color = TextWarmTaupe, fontSize = 10.sp)
                        )
                    }
                }

                Text(
                    text = "View Album",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = DeepEspressoPrimary
                    ),
                    modifier = Modifier.clickable { onViewJournalAlbumClick() }
                )
            }

            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(14.dp))

            // Camera and Gallery Shortcut Buttons Row
            Row(
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(10.dp)
            ) {
                // Take Photo (Camera) Button
                Button(
                    onClick = onCameraClick,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(48.dp)
                        .testTag("btn_take_bump_photo"),

                ) {

                        Icon(
                            imageVector = Icons.Default.CameraAlt,
                            contentDescription = "Camera",
                            tint = Color.White,
                            modifier = Modifier.size(18.dp)
                        )
                        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(6.dp))
                        Text(
                            text = stringResource(id = R.string.take_bump_photo),
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = Color.White
                            )
                        )
                    }
                }

                // Choose from Gallery Button
                OutlinedButton(
                    onClick = onGalleryClick,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .weight(1f)
                        .height(48.dp)
                        .testTag("btn_pick_bump_gallery"),

                            imageVector = Icons.Default.PhotoLibrary,

        }

@Composable
private fun SquircleThumbnail(
    title: String,
    emojiOrIcon: String,
    subtitle: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
    onClick: () -> Unit
) {
    Column(
        modifier = modifier
            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(20.dp))
            .background(SquircleItemBg)
            .clickable { onClick() }
            .padding(vertical = 14.dp, horizontal = 8.dp),
        horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
    ) {
        Box(
            modifier = Modifier
                .size(46.dp)
                .clip(RoundedCornerShape(14.dp))
                .background(CrispWhiteSurface),
            contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
        ) {
            Text(text = emojiOrIcon, fontSize = 24.sp)
        }
        Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
        Text(
            text = title,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelMedium.copy(fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold, color = TextDarkEspresso, fontSize = 11.sp),
            maxLines = 1
        )
        Text(
            text = subtitle,
            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(color = TextWarmTaupe, fontSize = 9.sp),
            maxLines = 1
        )
    }
}

@Composable
fun WeekDetailScreen(
    viewModel: PregnancyViewModel,
    onBack: () -> Unit
) {
    val currentWeek by viewModel.currentWeekNumber.collectAsState()
    val weekData by viewModel.currentWeekData.collectAsState()
    val sizeTheme by viewModel.selectedSizeTheme.collectAsState()
    val dueDateMillis by viewModel.dueDateMillis.collectAsState()

    var isFavorited by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }

    val comparison = weekData.getComparison(sizeTheme)
    val diffDays = ((dueDateMillis - System.currentTimeMillis()) / (1000L * 3600 * 24)).toInt().coerceAtLeast(0)

    Column(
        modifier = Modifier
            .fillMaxSize()
            .background(WarmCanvasBackground)
    ) {
        // Top Navigation Bar (matching Screen 2 "Cardigan" from screenshot)
        Row(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .statusBarsPadding()
                .padding(horizontal = 16.dp, vertical = 12.dp),
            verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
            horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.SpaceBetween
        ) {
            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                IconButton(
                    onClick = onBack,
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_week_back")
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack,
                        contentDescription = "Back",
                        tint = DeepEspressoPrimary
                    )
                }
                Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
                Text(
                    text = "Week $currentWeek",
                    style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                        fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                        color = TextDarkEspresso
                    )
                )
            }

            Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
                IconButton(
                    onClick = { isFavorited = !isFavorited },
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_favorite_milestone")
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = if (isFavorited) Icons.Default.Favorite else Icons.Default.FavoriteBorder,
                        contentDescription = "Favorite",
                        tint = DeepEspressoPrimary
                    )
                }
                IconButton(
                    onClick = { /* share */ },
                    modifier = Modifier.testTag("btn_share_milestone")
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        imageVector = Icons.Default.Share,
                        contentDescription = "Share",
                        tint = DeepEspressoPrimary
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Scrollable content
        LazyColumn(
            modifier = Modifier
                .weight(1f)
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(horizontal = 20.dp),
            contentPadding = PaddingValues(top = 4.dp, bottom = 16.dp),
            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(16.dp)
        ) {
            // Big Squircle Hero Card (matching huge photo card in Screen 2)
            item {
                Card(
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .fillMaxWidth()
                        .shadow(elevation = 6.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.06f))
                        .testTag("hero_squircle_card"),
                    shape = RoundedCornerShape(26.dp),
                    colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = SquircleItemBg)
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .fillMaxWidth()
                            .height(240.dp)
                            .padding(24.dp),
                        contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
                    ) {
                        Column(
                            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
                            verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
                        ) {
                            Text(
                                text = comparison.emoji,
                                fontSize = 84.sp
                            )
                            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(12.dp))
                            Text(
                                text = comparison.description,
                                style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
                                    fontWeight = FontWeight.SemiBold,
                                    color = TextDarkEspresso
                                )
                            )
                        }
                    }
                }
            }

            // Category Pill Badges (matching [Winter] [Autumn] from Screen 2)
            item {
                Row(
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                    horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
                ) {
                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .background(SoftToffeeSecondary)
                            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Trimester ${weekData.trimester}",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                fontSize = 11.sp
                            )
                        )
                    }

                    Box(
                        modifier = Modifier
                            .clip(RoundedCornerShape(12.dp))
                            .background(SoftToffeeSecondary)
                            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                    ) {
                        Text(
                            text = "Week $currentWeek of 40",
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = DeepEspressoPrimary,
                                fontSize = 11.sp
                            )
                        )
                    }

                    Box(

                            .clickable {

                                    SizeTheme.FRUITS -> SizeTheme.ANIMALS

                                    SizeTheme.SNACKS -> SizeTheme.SPORTS
                                    SizeTheme.SPORTS -> SizeTheme.FRUITS

                            }
                            .padding(horizontal = 12.dp, vertical = 6.dp)
                    ) {

                            text = sizeTheme.displayName,
                            style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                                fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                                color = DeepEspressoPrimary,

                    }
                }

                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                            letterSpacing = 1.sp,

                            color = TextWarmTaupe,

                        )

                        text = comparison.title,
                        style = MaterialTheme.typography.headlineLarge.copy(
                            fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                            color = TextDarkEspresso,
                            fontSize = 32.sp
                        )

@Composable
private fun StatPillBox(
    label: String,
    value: String,
    modifier: Modifier = Modifier
) {
    Card(
        modifier = modifier
            .shadow(elevation = 2.dp, shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp), spotColor = Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.04f)),
        shape = RoundedCornerShape(16.dp),
        colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(containerColor = CrispWhiteSurface)
    ) {
        Column(
            modifier = Modifier
                .fillMaxWidth()
                .padding(vertical = 12.dp, horizontal = 8.dp),
            horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally
        ) {
            Text(
                text = label,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.labelSmall.copy(
                    letterSpacing = 0.8.sp,
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = TextWarmTaupe,
                    fontSize = 9.sp
                )
            )
            Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(2.dp))
            Text(
                text = value,
                style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium.copy(
                    fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
                    color = TextDarkEspresso,
                    fontSize = 15.sp
                )
            )
        }
    }
}

private val DarkColorScheme = darkColorScheme(
    primary = Color(0xFFE5B896),
    onPrimary = Color(0xFF3B1A04),
    primaryContainer = Color(0xFF5A2C0D),
    onPrimaryContainer = SoftToffeeSecondary,
    secondary = CaramelSecondary,
    onSecondary = Color(0xFF381F08),
    secondaryContainer = Color(0xFF563518),
    onSecondaryContainer = SoftToffeeContainer,
    tertiary = SoftLavenderDark,
    onTertiary = Color(0xFF38230F),
    tertiaryContainer = Color(0xFF563A21),
    onTertiaryContainer = Color(0xFFF8EDE3),
    background = DarkBackground,
    onBackground = TextLight,
    surface = DarkSurface,
    onSurface = TextLight,
    surfaceVariant = DarkSurfaceVariant,
    onSurfaceVariant = TextLightSubtle
)

private val LightColorScheme = lightColorScheme(
    primary = CaramelPrimary, // #9E5727
    onPrimary = Color.White,
    primaryContainer = SoftToffeeContainer,
    onPrimaryContainer = CaramelPrimary,
    secondary = CaramelSecondary, // #DDA15E
    onSecondary = TextEspresso,
    secondaryContainer = SoftToffeeContainer,
    onSecondaryContainer = CaramelPrimary,
    tertiary = SoftLavender,
    onTertiary = Color.White,
    tertiaryContainer = Color(0xFFF8EDE3),
    onTertiaryContainer = CaramelPrimary,
    background = WarmSurfaceBackground, // #FDFBF7
    onBackground = TextEspresso,        // #2D1B10
    surface = CrispWhiteSurface,
    onSurface = TextEspresso,           // #2D1B10
    surfaceVariant = SoftToffeeContainer,
    onSurfaceVariant = TextWarmTaupe
)

@Composable
fun MamanBebeTheme(
    darkTheme: Boolean = isSystemInDarkTheme(),
    dynamicColor: Boolean = false,
    content: @Composable () -> Unit
) {
    val colorScheme = when {
        dynamicColor && Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S -> {
            val context = LocalContext.current
            if (darkTheme) dynamicDarkColorScheme(context) else dynamicLightColorScheme(context)
        }
        darkTheme -> DarkColorScheme
        else -> LightColorScheme
    }

    MaterialTheme(
        colorScheme = colorScheme,
        typography = Typography,
        shapes = Shapes,
        content = content
    )
}

    init {
