    private fun build14DayCalendarStrip() {
        val list = mutableListOf<CalendarDay>()
        val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
        cal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, -6) // 6 days before today

        val dayFmt = SimpleDateFormat("d", Locale.getDefault())
        val dayOfWeekFmt = SimpleDateFormat("EEE", Locale.getDefault())
        val fullFmt = SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy", Locale.getDefault())
        val todayStr = fullFmt.format(Date())

        for (i in 0 until 14) {
            val date = cal.time
            val dateStr = fullFmt.format(date)
            list.add(
                CalendarDay(
                    dateString = dateStr,
                    dayOfMonth = dayFmt.format(date),
                    dayOfWeek = dayOfWeekFmt.format(date),
                    fullDate = date,
                    isToday = dateStr == todayStr
                )
            )
            cal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, 1)
        }
        _calendarStrip.value = list
    }

    private fun bootstrapDefaultData() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            try {
                if (dao.getChecklistCount() == 0) {
                    val entities = ChecklistData.defaultItems.map {
                        ChecklistItemEntity(
                            category = it.category,
                            title = it.title,
                            isChecked = false,
                            isCustom = false
                        )
                    }
                    dao.insertChecklistItems(entities)
                }

                launch {
                    try {
                        dao.getAllAppointments().collect { list ->
                            if (list.isEmpty()) {
                                PrenatalAgendaData.defaultAgenda.forEach { agenda ->
                                    dao.insertAppointment(
                                        AppointmentEntity(
                                            title = agenda.title,
                                            weekSuggested = agenda.targetWeek,
                                            dateTimestamp = System.currentTimeMillis() + (agenda.targetWeek - _currentWeekNumber.value).coerceAtLeast(1) * 7L * 24 * 3600 * 1000,
                                            doctorName = "Dr. Laurent (OB/GYN)",
                                            location = "Maternal Health Center",
                                            notes = agenda.description,
                                            isCompleted = false
                                        )
                                    )
                                }
                            }
                        }
                    } catch (e: Exception) {
                        android.util.Log.e("PregnancyViewModel", "Error collecting appointments", e)
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                android.util.Log.e("PregnancyViewModel", "Error bootstrapping default data", e)
            }
        }
    }

    // Dynamic Due Date Engine
    fun updateDueDate(newDueDateMillis: Long) {
        _dueDateMillis.value = newDueDateMillis
        prefs.edit().putLong("pref_due_date", newDueDateMillis).apply()
        val diffDays = ((newDueDateMillis - System.currentTimeMillis()) / (1000L * 3600 * 24)).toInt()
        val daysElapsed = 280 - diffDays
        val calculatedWeek = (daysElapsed / 7).coerceIn(1, 42)
        setWeek(calculatedWeek)
    }

    fun setWeek(week: Int) {
        val clamped = week.coerceIn(1, 42)
        _currentWeekNumber.value = clamped
        _currentWeekData.value = PregnancyWeeksData.getWeek(clamped)
        prefs.edit().putInt("pref_current_week", clamped).apply()
    }

    fun toggleTwinMode() {
        val updated = !_isTwinMode.value
        _isTwinMode.value = updated
        prefs.edit().putBoolean("pref_is_twin", updated).apply()
    }

    fun setSizeTheme(theme: SizeTheme) {
        _selectedSizeTheme.value = theme
        prefs.edit().putString("pref_size_theme", theme.name).apply()
    }

    fun selectCalendarDate(dateString: String) {
        _selectedCalendarDate.value = dateString
    }

    fun selectTab(tab: ScreenTab) {
        _currentTab.value = tab
    }

    // Privacy PIN & Security Methods
    fun setPin(pin: String) {
        prefs.edit()
            .putString("pref_pin_code", pin)
            .putBoolean("pref_pin_enabled", true)
            .apply()
        _isPinEnabled.value = true
        _isAppLocked.value = false
        _pinResetStatus.value = "PIN protection activated successfully"
    }

    fun disablePin() {
        prefs.edit()
            .remove("pref_pin_code")
            .putBoolean("pref_pin_enabled", false)
            .apply()
        _isPinEnabled.value = false
        _isAppLocked.value = false
        _pinResetStatus.value = "PIN protection disabled"
    }

    fun verifyPin(inputPin: String): Boolean {
        val savedPin = prefs.getString("pref_pin_code", "") ?: ""
        return inputPin == savedPin
    }

    fun unlockAppWithPin(inputPin: String): Boolean {
        val savedPin = prefs.getString("pref_pin_code", "") ?: ""
        return if (inputPin == savedPin) {
            _isAppLocked.value = false
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    fun unlockWithBiometric() {
        _isAppLocked.value = false
    }

    fun lockApp() {
        if (_isPinEnabled.value) {
            _isAppLocked.value = true
        }
    }

    fun requestPinResetCode() {
        val code = (100000..999999).random().toString()
        _pinResetCode.value = code
        _pinResetStatus.value = "Reset code ($code) sent to ${_userEmail.value}"
    }

    fun verifyResetCodeAndResetPin(code: String, newPin: String): Boolean {
        val currentCode = _pinResetCode.value
        return if (currentCode != null && code.trim() == currentCode.trim()) {
            setPin(newPin)
            _pinResetCode.value = null
            _pinResetStatus.value = "PIN has been reset successfully"
            true
        } else {
            false
        }
    }

    fun clearPinResetStatus() {
        _pinResetStatus.value = null
    }

    // Bump Milestone Photo Persistence
    fun saveBumpPhoto(bitmap: Bitmap, week: Int, title: String = "", notes: String = "") {
        viewModelScope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val dir = java.io.File(getApplication<Application>().filesDir, "bump_photos")
                if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs()
                val filename = "bump_wk_${week}_${System.currentTimeMillis()}.jpg"
                val file = java.io.File(dir, filename)
                java.io.FileOutputStream(file).use { out ->
                    bitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 92, out)
                }
                val dateFmt = SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
                dao.insertJournalEntry(
                    JournalEntryEntity(
                        weekNumber = week,
                        dateString = dateFmt,
                        timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                        title = title.ifBlank { "Week $week Bump Photo" },
                        reflection = notes.ifBlank { "Captured on $dateFmt • Week $week" },
                        photoUri = file.absolutePath
                    )
                )
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
            }
        }
    }

    fun saveBumpPhotoUri(uriString: String, week: Int, title: String = "", notes: String = "") {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val dateFmt = SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())
            dao.insertJournalEntry(
                JournalEntryEntity(
                    weekNumber = week,
                    dateString = dateFmt,
                    timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                    title = title.ifBlank { "Week $week Bump Photo" },
                    reflection = notes.ifBlank { "Added from gallery on $dateFmt" },
                    photoUri = uriString
                )
            )
        }
    }

    // Health Connect Actions
    fun refreshHealthConnectState() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            _healthConnectStatus.value = healthConnectManager.checkAvailability()
            val hasPerms = healthConnectManager.hasAllPermissions()
            _hasHealthPermissions.value = hasPerms
            if (hasPerms) {
                loadHealthConnectData()
            }
        }
    }

    fun onHealthPermissionsResult(granted: Boolean) {
        _hasHealthPermissions.value = granted
        if (granted) {
            _healthConnectMessage.value = "Health Connect connected securely!"
            loadHealthConnectData()
        } else {
            _healthConnectMessage.value = "Health Connect permissions were not granted."
        }
    }

    fun clearHealthMessage() {
        _healthConnectMessage.value = null
    }

    private fun loadHealthConnectData() {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            healthConnectManager.readRecentWeights(days = 60).onSuccess { list ->
                _syncedWeights.value = list
            }
            healthConnectManager.readRecentSleepSessions(days = 14).onSuccess { list ->
                _syncedSleep.value = list
            }
        }
    }

    // Kick Counter
    fun registerKick() {
        _kickCount.value += 1
        triggerHaptic()
        if (!_isKickTimerRunning.value) {
            startKickTimer()
        }
    }

    fun startKickTimer() {
        _isKickTimerRunning.value = true
        kickTimerJob?.cancel()
        kickTimerJob = viewModelScope.launch {
            while (_isKickTimerRunning.value) {
                delay(1000)
                _kickTimerSeconds.value += 1
            }
        }
    }

    fun pauseKickTimer() {
        _isKickTimerRunning.value = false
        kickTimerJob?.cancel()
    }

    fun resetKickCounter() {
        pauseKickTimer()
        _kickCount.value = 0
        _kickTimerSeconds.value = 0L
    }

    fun saveKickSession(notes: String = "") {
        val count = _kickCount.value
        val duration = _kickTimerSeconds.value
        val reached = count >= 10
        viewModelScope.launch {
            dao.insertKickSession(
                KickSessionEntity(
                    timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                    durationSeconds = duration,
                    kickCount = count,
                    targetReached = reached,
                    notes = notes
                )
            )
            resetKickCounter()
        }
    }

    private fun triggerHaptic() {
        try {
            val app = getApplication<Application>()
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.S) {
                val vibratorManager = app.getSystemService(VibratorManager::class.java)
                vibratorManager?.defaultVibrator?.vibrate(
                    VibrationEffect.createPredefined(VibrationEffect.EFFECT_CLICK)
                )
            } else {
                @Suppress("DEPRECATION")
                val vibrator = app.getSystemService(Vibrator::class.java)
                vibrator?.vibrate(VibrationEffect.createOneShot(50, VibrationEffect.DEFAULT_AMPLITUDE))
            }
        } catch (_: Exception) {}
    }

    // Daily Pregnancy Health Log
    fun incrementWater() {
        _todayWaterGlasses.value += 1
        if (_hasHealthPermissions.value) {
            viewModelScope.launch {
                healthConnectManager.writeHydration(0.25)
            }
        }
    }

    fun decrementWater() {
        if (_todayWaterGlasses.value > 0) {
            _todayWaterGlasses.value -= 1
        }
    }

    fun setWeight(weight: Double) {
        _todayWeightKg.value = weight
    }

    fun setTodayWeight(weight: Double) = setWeight(weight)

    fun setTodayWater(glasses: Int) {
        _todayWaterGlasses.value = glasses.coerceAtLeast(0)
    }

    fun setMood(mood: String) {
        _todayMood.value = mood
    }

    fun setTodayMood(mood: String) = setMood(mood)

    fun saveDailyHealthLog(bp: String = "", notes: String = "") = saveTodayLog(bp, notes)

    fun toggleSymptom(symptom: String) {
        toggleSymptomWithSeverity(symptom)
    }

    fun toggleSymptomWithSeverity(symptom: String, severity: String = "Mild") {
        val current = _todaySymptoms.value.toMutableSet()
        val severities = _symptomSeverities.value.toMutableMap()
        if (symptom in current) {
            current.remove(symptom)
            severities.remove(symptom)
        } else {
            current.add(symptom)
            severities[symptom] = severity
        }
        _todaySymptoms.value = current
        _symptomSeverities.value = severities
    }

    fun saveTodayLog(bp: String = "", notes: String = "") {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val weight = _todayWeightKg.value
            val glasses = _todayWaterGlasses.value
            val mood = _todayMood.value
            val symptomsWithSeverityStr = _todaySymptoms.value.joinToString(", ") { symptom ->
                val sev = _symptomSeverities.value[symptom] ?: "Mild"
                "$symptom ($sev)"
            }

            var synced = false
            if (_hasHealthPermissions.value && weight > 0) {
                val result = healthConnectManager.writeWeight(weight)
                synced = result.isSuccess
                if (synced) loadHealthConnectData()
            }

            dao.insertOrUpdatePregnancyLog(
                PregnancyLogEntity(
                    dateString = _selectedCalendarDate.value,
                    timestamp = System.currentTimeMillis(),
                    weightKg = weight,
                    waterGlasses = glasses,
                    mood = mood,
                    symptoms = symptomsWithSeverityStr,
                    bloodPressure = bp,
                    notes = notes,
                    syncedHealthConnect = synced
                )
            )
            _healthConnectMessage.value = if (synced) "Saved & synced with Health Connect!" else "Daily entry saved!"
        }
    }

    // Baby Postpartum Logger
    fun toggleNursingTimer(side: String) {
        if (_isNursingTimerRunning.value) {
            if (_nursingSide.value == side) {
                _isNursingTimerRunning.value = false
                nursingTimerJob?.cancel()
            } else {
                _nursingSide.value = side
            }
        } else {
            _nursingSide.value = side
            _isNursingTimerRunning.value = true
            nursingTimerJob?.cancel()
            nursingTimerJob = viewModelScope.launch {
                while (_isNursingTimerRunning.value) {
                    delay(1000)
                    _nursingDurationSeconds.value += 1
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun saveNursingSession(notes: String = "") {
        _isNursingTimerRunning.value = false
        nursingTimerJob?.cancel()
        val duration = _nursingDurationSeconds.value
        val side = _nursingSide.value
        viewModelScope.launch {
            dao.insertBabyLog(
                BabyLogEntity(
                    logType = "NURSING",
                    detail = "$side Breast",
                    durationSeconds = duration,
                    amountMl = 0,
                    notes = notes
                )
            )
            _nursingDurationSeconds.value = 0L
        }
    }

    fun logBottleFeeding(amountMl: Int, formula: Boolean, notes: String = "") {

    }

    fun logDiaper(type: String, notes: String = "") {

                    logType = "DIAPER",

    fun logSleepSession(durationMinutes: Long, isBaby: Boolean = true, notes: String = "") {
        viewModelScope.launch {
            val endTime = Instant.now()
            val startTime = endTime.minusSeconds(durationMinutes * 60)
            var synced = false

            if (_hasHealthPermissions.value) {
                val title = if (isBaby) "Baby Nap" else "Maternal Sleep"
                val res = healthConnectManager.writeSleepSession(startTime, endTime, title)
                synced = res.isSuccess
                if (synced) loadHealthConnectData()
            }

            dao.insertBabyLog(
                BabyLogEntity(
                    logType = "SLEEP",
                    detail = if (isBaby) "Baby Sleep ($durationMinutes min)" else "Mom Sleep ($durationMinutes min)",
                    durationSeconds = durationMinutes * 60,
                    notes = notes,
                    syncedHealthConnect = synced
                )
            )
            _healthConnectMessage.value = if (synced) "Sleep synced to Health Connect!" else "Sleep recorded!"
        }
    }

    }

    fun savePhotoToDeviceGallery(context: Context, photoUriStr: String?) {
        if (photoUriStr.isNullOrBlank()) return
        viewModelScope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val uri = Uri.parse(photoUriStr)
                val inputStream = context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri) ?: return@launch
                val bitmap = android.graphics.BitmapFactory.decodeStream(inputStream)
                inputStream.close()

                val filename = "MamanBebe_${System.currentTimeMillis()}.jpg"
                var fos: java.io.OutputStream? = null
                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                    val resolver = context.contentResolver
                    val contentValues = android.content.ContentValues().apply {
                        put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME, filename)
                        put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.MIME_TYPE, "image/jpeg")
                        put(android.provider.MediaStore.MediaColumns.RELATIVE_PATH, android.os.Environment.DIRECTORY_PICTURES + "/MamanBebe")
                    }
                    val imageUri = resolver.insert(android.provider.MediaStore.Images.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, contentValues)
                    if (imageUri != null) {
                        fos = resolver.openOutputStream(imageUri)
                    }
                } else {
                    val imagesDir = android.os.Environment.getExternalStoragePublicDirectory(android.os.Environment.DIRECTORY_PICTURES)
                    val appDir = java.io.File(imagesDir, "MamanBebe")
                    if (!appDir.exists()) appDir.mkdirs()
                    val image = java.io.File(appDir, filename)
                    fos = java.io.FileOutputStream(image)
                }
                fos?.use {
                    bitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 95, it)
                    android.os.Handler(context.mainLooper).post {
                        android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Photo saved to device Gallery!", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    }
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                android.os.Handler(context.mainLooper).post {
                    android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Failed to save photo: ${e.localizedMessage}", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fun sharePhoto(context: Context, photoUriStr: String?, caption: String) {
        if (photoUriStr.isNullOrBlank()) return
        try {
            val uri = Uri.parse(photoUriStr)
            val intent = android.content.Intent(android.content.Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                type = "image/jpeg"
                putExtra(android.content.Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
                putExtra(android.content.Intent.EXTRA_TEXT, "Maman & Bébé Memory: $caption")
                addFlags(android.content.Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
            }
            context.startActivity(android.content.Intent.createChooser(intent, "Share Photo via"))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Failed to share photo: ${e.localizedMessage}", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    fun cropAndSavePhoto(
        context: Context,
        originalUriStr: String,
        aspectRatio: String, // "1:1", "4:5", "16:9", "ORIGINAL"
        rotationAngle: Float,
        onSuccess: (String) -> Unit
    ) {
        viewModelScope.launch(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.IO) {
            try {
                val uri = Uri.parse(originalUriStr)
                val inputStream = if (originalUriStr.startsWith("/")) {
                    java.io.FileInputStream(java.io.File(originalUriStr))
                } else {
                    context.contentResolver.openInputStream(uri)
                } ?: return@launch

                val originalBitmap = android.graphics.BitmapFactory.decodeStream(inputStream)
                inputStream.close()

                if (originalBitmap == null) {
                    withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.Main) {
                        android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Cannot read photo bitmap to crop", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                    }
                    return@launch
                }

                // Apply rotation
                val matrix = android.graphics.Matrix().apply {
                    if (rotationAngle != 0f) postRotate(rotationAngle)
                }
                val rotatedBitmap = android.graphics.Bitmap.createBitmap(
                    originalBitmap, 0, 0, originalBitmap.width, originalBitmap.height, matrix, true
                )

                // Calculate crop bounds based on chosen aspect ratio
                val targetRatio = when (aspectRatio) {
                    "1:1" -> 1.0f
                    "4:5" -> 0.8f
                    "16:9" -> 16f / 9f
                    else -> rotatedBitmap.width.toFloat() / rotatedBitmap.height.toFloat()
                }

                val currentRatio = rotatedBitmap.width.toFloat() / rotatedBitmap.height.toFloat()
                var cropW = rotatedBitmap.width
                var cropH = rotatedBitmap.height
                var startX = 0
                var startY = 0

                if (currentRatio > targetRatio) {
                    cropW = (rotatedBitmap.height * targetRatio).toInt().coerceAtMost(rotatedBitmap.width)
                    startX = (rotatedBitmap.width - cropW) / 2
                } else if (currentRatio < targetRatio) {
                    cropH = (rotatedBitmap.width / targetRatio).toInt().coerceAtMost(rotatedBitmap.height)
                    startY = (rotatedBitmap.height - cropH) / 2
                }

                val croppedBitmap = android.graphics.Bitmap.createBitmap(
                    rotatedBitmap, startX, startY, cropW, cropH
                )

                val dir = java.io.File(getApplication<Application>().filesDir, "bump_photos")
                if (!dir.exists()) dir.mkdirs()
                val croppedFile = java.io.File(dir, "crop_${System.currentTimeMillis()}.jpg")
                java.io.FileOutputStream(croppedFile).use { out ->
                    croppedBitmap.compress(Bitmap.CompressFormat.JPEG, 92, out)
                }

                withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.Main) {
                    onSuccess(croppedFile.absolutePath)
                    android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Photo cropped & saved!", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            } catch (e: Exception) {
                e.printStackTrace()
                withContext(kotlinx.coroutines.Dispatchers.Main) {
                    android.widget.Toast.makeText(context, "Failed to crop: ${e.localizedMessage}", android.widget.Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // AI Assistant
    fun askAssistant(prompt: String) {
        if (prompt.isBlank()) return
        val userMsg = ChatMessage(text = prompt, isUser = true)
        _chatMessages.value = _chatMessages.value + userMsg
        _isAssistantThinking.value = true

        viewModelScope.launch {
            val result = assistantClient.askAssistant(
                prompt = prompt,
                currentWeek = _currentWeekNumber.value
            )
            _isAssistantThinking.value = false
            val replyText = result.getOrElse {
                "I am here to support you. Please make sure to discuss any medical concerns with your obstetrician or midwife."
            }
            _chatMessages.value = _chatMessages.value + ChatMessage(text = replyText, isUser = false)
        }
    }
}

package com.example.util

import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.graphics.Canvas
import android.graphics.Color
import android.graphics.Paint
import android.graphics.RectF
import android.graphics.Typeface
import android.graphics.pdf.PdfDocument
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import com.example.data.local.AppointmentEntity
import com.example.data.local.MedicalContactEntity
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

object CareDirectoryPdfExporter {

    private const val PAGE_WIDTH = 595 // Standard A4 width in points
    private const val PAGE_HEIGHT = 842 // Standard A4 height in points
    private const val MARGIN = 40f
    private const val CONTENT_WIDTH = PAGE_WIDTH - (MARGIN * 2)

    /**
     * Generates a polished PDF document containing the patient's Care Directory contacts
     * and scheduled prenatal clinical visits.
     */
    fun generateCareDirectoryPdf(
        context: Context,
        currentWeek: Int,
        dueDateMillis: Long,
        appointments: List<AppointmentEntity>,
        contacts: List<MedicalContactEntity>
    ): File? {
        val document = PdfDocument()

        val paint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
        val textPaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)
        val linePaint = Paint(Paint.ANTI_ALIAS_FLAG)

        var pageNumber = 1
        var pageInfo = PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(PAGE_WIDTH, PAGE_HEIGHT, pageNumber).create()
        var page = document.startPage(pageInfo)
        var canvas = page.canvas

        var currentY = MARGIN

        fun drawHeader() {
            // Top Accent Banner
            paint.color = Color.rgb(217, 90, 101) // Warm Rose Primary
            paint.style = Paint.Style.FILL
            val headerRect = RectF(MARGIN, currentY, MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH, currentY + 70f)
            canvas.drawRoundRect(headerRect, 8f, 8f, paint)

            // Header Title
            textPaint.color = Color.WHITE
            textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
            textPaint.textSize = 16f
            canvas.drawText("MAMAN & BÉBÉ PREGNANCY CARE DIRECTORY", MARGIN + 16f, currentY + 28f, textPaint)

            textPaint.textSize = 10f
            textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.NORMAL)
            canvas.drawText("Medical Contacts, Clinical Visits & Prenatal Appointment Summary", MARGIN + 16f, currentY + 46f, textPaint)

            currentY += 82f

            // Patient Summary Info Box
            paint.color = Color.rgb(255, 245, 246) // Soft Rose Tint
            val summaryBox = RectF(MARGIN, currentY, MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH, currentY + 52f)
            canvas.drawRoundRect(summaryBox, 6f, 6f, paint)

            paint.color = Color.rgb(245, 204, 210)
            paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
            paint.strokeWidth = 1f
            canvas.drawRoundRect(summaryBox, 6f, 6f, paint)
            paint.style = Paint.Style.FILL

            val dueDateFormatted = SimpleDateFormat("MMMM dd, yyyy", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(dueDateMillis))
            val exportedDate = SimpleDateFormat("MMM dd, yyyy • hh:mm a", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date())

            textPaint.color = Color.rgb(30, 20, 12)
            textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
            textPaint.textSize = 10f
            canvas.drawText("Gestational Age: Week $currentWeek of 40", MARGIN + 12f, currentY + 20f, textPaint)
            canvas.drawText("Estimated Due Date: $dueDateFormatted", MARGIN + 12f, currentY + 38f, textPaint)

            textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.NORMAL)
            textPaint.color = Color.rgb(110, 86, 71)
            canvas.drawText("Generated: $exportedDate", MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH - 180f, currentY + 20f, textPaint)
            canvas.drawText("Purpose: Clinical & Medical Consultations", MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH - 210f, currentY + 38f, textPaint)

            currentY += 66f
        }

        fun drawFooter(pageNum: Int) {
            linePaint.color = Color.rgb(230, 220, 215)
            linePaint.strokeWidth = 1f
            canvas.drawLine(MARGIN, PAGE_HEIGHT - 32f, MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH, PAGE_HEIGHT - 32f, linePaint)

            textPaint.color = Color.rgb(130, 115, 105)
            textPaint.textSize = 8.5f
            textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.NORMAL)
            canvas.drawText("Maman & Bébé • Confidential Prenatal Medical Summary", MARGIN, PAGE_HEIGHT - 20f, textPaint)

            val pageStr = "Page $pageNum"
            val textWidth = textPaint.measureText(pageStr)
            canvas.drawText(pageStr, MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH - textWidth, PAGE_HEIGHT - 20f, textPaint)
        }

        fun checkNewPage(neededHeight: Float) {
            if (currentY + neededHeight > PAGE_HEIGHT - 50f) {
                drawFooter(pageNumber)
                document.finishPage(page)
                pageNumber++
                pageInfo = PdfDocument.PageInfo.Builder(PAGE_WIDTH, PAGE_HEIGHT, pageNumber).create()
                page = document.startPage(pageInfo)
                canvas = page.canvas
                currentY = MARGIN + 20f
            }
        }

        // Draw initial page header
        drawHeader()

        // 1. SECTION: MEDICAL CONTACTS DIRECTORY
        checkNewPage(40f)
        paint.color = Color.rgb(217, 90, 101)
        canvas.drawRect(MARGIN, currentY, MARGIN + 4f, currentY + 16f, paint)

        textPaint.color = Color.rgb(30, 20, 12)
        textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
        textPaint.textSize = 12f
        canvas.drawText("1. MEDICAL DIRECTORY & CARE CONTACTS", MARGIN + 10f, currentY + 13f, textPaint)
        currentY += 24f

        if (contacts.isEmpty()) {
            checkNewPage(24f)
            textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.ITALIC)
            textPaint.color = Color.GRAY
            textPaint.textSize = 9.5f
            canvas.drawText("No contacts registered. Add your preferred doctors, clinic, and pharmacy in Maman & Bébé.", MARGIN + 8f, currentY + 12f, textPaint)
            currentY += 24f
        } else {
            for (contact in contacts) {
                checkNewPage(56f)

                // Contact card background
                paint.color = Color.rgb(250, 250, 252)
                val cardRect = RectF(MARGIN, currentY, MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH, currentY + 50f)
                canvas.drawRoundRect(cardRect, 4f, 4f, paint)

                paint.color = Color.rgb(230, 230, 235)
                paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                paint.strokeWidth = 0.8f
                canvas.drawRoundRect(cardRect, 4f, 4f, paint)
                paint.style = Paint.Style.FILL

                // Contact Type Badge
                paint.color = when (contact.type.uppercase()) {
                    "DOCTOR" -> Color.rgb(226, 109, 119)
                    "PHARMACY" -> Color.rgb(77, 124, 102)
                    "CLINIC", "HOSPITAL" -> Color.rgb(138, 104, 176)
                    else -> Color.rgb(110, 86, 71)
                }
                val badgeRect = RectF(MARGIN + 8f, currentY + 8f, MARGIN + 72f, currentY + 22f)
                canvas.drawRoundRect(badgeRect, 3f, 3f, paint)

                textPaint.color = Color.WHITE
                textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                textPaint.textSize = 8f
                canvas.drawText(contact.type.uppercase(), MARGIN + 12f, currentY + 18f, textPaint)

                // Contact Name
                textPaint.color = Color.rgb(30, 20, 12)
                textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                textPaint.textSize = 10.5f
                canvas.drawText(contact.name, MARGIN + 78f, currentY + 18f, textPaint)

                // Phone
                if (contact.phone.isNotBlank()) {
                    textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.NORMAL)
                    textPaint.color = Color.rgb(217, 90, 101)
                    textPaint.textSize = 9.5f
                    canvas.drawText("Tel: ${contact.phone}", MARGIN + 8f, currentY + 36f, textPaint)
                }

                // Address & Notes
                var infoLine = ""
                if (contact.address.isNotBlank()) infoLine += "Address: ${contact.address}  "
                if (contact.notes.isNotBlank()) infoLine += "• Notes: ${contact.notes}"

                if (infoLine.isNotBlank()) {
                    textPaint.color = Color.rgb(90, 80, 75)
                    textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.NORMAL)
                    textPaint.textSize = 8.5f
                    val truncated = if (infoLine.length > 75) infoLine.take(72) + "..." else infoLine
                    canvas.drawText(truncated, MARGIN + 130f, currentY + 36f, textPaint)
                }

                currentY += 56f
            }
        }

        currentY += 10f

        // 2. SECTION: CLINICAL VISITS & APPOINTMENTS
        checkNewPage(40f)
        paint.color = Color.rgb(217, 90, 101)
        canvas.drawRect(MARGIN, currentY, MARGIN + 4f, currentY + 16f, paint)

        textPaint.color = Color.rgb(30, 20, 12)
        textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
        textPaint.textSize = 12f
        canvas.drawText("2. PRENATAL CARE & SCHEDULED CLINICAL VISITS", MARGIN + 10f, currentY + 13f, textPaint)
        currentY += 24f

        if (appointments.isEmpty()) {
            checkNewPage(24f)
            textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.ITALIC)
            textPaint.color = Color.GRAY
            textPaint.textSize = 9.5f
            canvas.drawText("No upcoming clinical appointments logged.", MARGIN + 8f, currentY + 12f, textPaint)
            currentY += 24f
        } else {
            for (app in appointments) {
                checkNewPage(60f)

                paint.color = if (app.isCompleted) Color.rgb(246, 252, 248) else Color.rgb(255, 255, 255)
                val appRect = RectF(MARGIN, currentY, MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH, currentY + 54f)
                canvas.drawRoundRect(appRect, 4f, 4f, paint)

                paint.color = Color.rgb(230, 225, 220)
                paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
                paint.strokeWidth = 0.8f
                canvas.drawRoundRect(appRect, 4f, 4f, paint)
                paint.style = Paint.Style.FILL

                // Title & Status
                textPaint.color = Color.rgb(30, 20, 12)
                textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
                textPaint.textSize = 10.5f
                canvas.drawText(app.title, MARGIN + 10f, currentY + 18f, textPaint)

                val statusText = if (app.isCompleted) "[COMPLETED]" else "[SCHEDULED]"
                textPaint.color = if (app.isCompleted) Color.rgb(77, 124, 102) else Color.rgb(217, 90, 101)
                textPaint.textSize = 8.5f
                val statusWidth = textPaint.measureText(statusText)
                canvas.drawText(statusText, MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH - statusWidth - 10f, currentY + 18f, textPaint)

                // Date & Doctor Info
                val dateStr = SimpleDateFormat("EEEE, MMMM dd, yyyy", Locale.getDefault()).format(Date(app.dateTimestamp))
                textPaint.color = Color.rgb(90, 75, 65)
                textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.NORMAL)
                textPaint.textSize = 9f
                canvas.drawText("Date: $dateStr", MARGIN + 10f, currentY + 34f, textPaint)

                val doctorDetails = "Physician: Dr. ${app.doctorName.ifBlank { "Assigned Specialist" }} • Location: ${app.location.ifBlank { "Maternity Clinic" }}"
                canvas.drawText(doctorDetails, MARGIN + 10f, currentY + 47f, textPaint)

                currentY += 60f
            }
        }

        currentY += 12f

        // 3. SECTION: PHYSICIAN CLINICAL CONSULTATION NOTES
        checkNewPage(120f)
        paint.color = Color.rgb(217, 90, 101)
        canvas.drawRect(MARGIN, currentY, MARGIN + 4f, currentY + 16f, paint)

        textPaint.color = Color.rgb(30, 20, 12)
        textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.BOLD)
        textPaint.textSize = 12f
        canvas.drawText("3. CLINICAL CONSULTATION NOTES & PHYSICIAN OBSERVATIONS", MARGIN + 10f, currentY + 13f, textPaint)
        currentY += 24f

        // Lined notes box for handwritten doctor notes during appointment
        paint.color = Color.rgb(255, 255, 255)
        val notesRect = RectF(MARGIN, currentY, MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH, currentY + 100f)
        canvas.drawRoundRect(notesRect, 4f, 4f, paint)

        paint.color = Color.rgb(220, 215, 210)
        paint.style = Paint.Style.STROKE
        paint.strokeWidth = 0.8f
        canvas.drawRoundRect(notesRect, 4f, 4f, paint)

        // Draw note lines for Doctor to handwrite Blood Pressure, Weight, Recommendations
        linePaint.color = Color.rgb(235, 230, 225)
        linePaint.strokeWidth = 0.7f
        var lineY = currentY + 25f
        while (lineY < currentY + 95f) {
            canvas.drawLine(MARGIN + 10f, lineY, MARGIN + CONTENT_WIDTH - 10f, lineY, linePaint)
            lineY += 24f
        }

        textPaint.color = Color.rgb(160, 150, 140)
        textPaint.textSize = 8.5f
        textPaint.typeface = Typeface.create(Typeface.DEFAULT, Typeface.ITALIC)
        canvas.drawText("(Handwritten clinical observations, blood pressure, fundal height, ultrasound notes & instructions)", MARGIN + 12f, currentY + 16f, textPaint)

        currentY += 110f

        // Finish last page footer
        drawFooter(pageNumber)
        document.finishPage(page)

        // Write to cache file
        return try {
            val exportDir = File(context.cacheDir, "exports")
            if (!exportDir.exists()) exportDir.mkdirs()
            val pdfFile = File(exportDir, "Maman_Care_Directory_Week_${currentWeek}.pdf")
            val outputStream = FileOutputStream(pdfFile)
            document.writeTo(outputStream)
            outputStream.flush()
            outputStream.close()
            document.close()
            pdfFile
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            document.close()
            null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Opens the standard Android Share sheet with the generated medical PDF document.
     */
    fun sharePdf(context: Context, pdfFile: File) {
        try {
            val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(
                context,
                "${context.packageName}.fileprovider",
                pdfFile
            )

            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                type = "application/pdf"
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, "Maman & Bébé Care Directory & Medical Appointment Summary")
                putExtra(
                    Intent.EXTRA_TEXT,
                    "Here is my prenatal care directory, contact list, and scheduled medical appointments exported from Maman & Bébé."
                )
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
            }

            context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, "Share Care Directory PDF via"))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            Toast.makeText(context, "Failed to share PDF: ${e.localizedMessage}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    /**
     * Opens the PDF directly in a PDF viewer app on the device.
     */
    fun viewPdf(context: Context, pdfFile: File) {
        try {
            val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(
                context,
                "${context.packageName}.fileprovider",
                pdfFile
            )

            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW).apply {
                setDataAndType(uri, "application/pdf")
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK)
            }

            context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, "Open Medical PDF with"))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            // Fallback to sharing if no dedicated viewer is installed
            sharePdf(context, pdfFile)
        }
    }
}

FILE 53/77: app/src/main/java/com/example/util/MamanDataTextExporter.kt
================================================================================

package com.example.util

import android.content.ClipData
import android.content.ClipboardManager
import android.content.Context
import android.content.Intent
import android.widget.Toast
import androidx.core.content.FileProvider
import com.example.data.local.AppointmentEntity
import com.example.data.local.BabyLogEntity
import com.example.data.local.ChecklistItemEntity
import com.example.data.local.JournalEntryEntity
import com.example.data.local.KickSessionEntity
import com.example.data.local.MedicalContactEntity
import com.example.data.local.PregnancyLogEntity
import java.io.File
import java.io.FileOutputStream
import java.text.SimpleDateFormat
import java.util.Date
import java.util.Locale

object MamanDataTextExporter {

    private val dateFormat = SimpleDateFormat("yyyy-MM-dd HH:mm", Locale.getDefault())
    private val dateOnlyFormat = SimpleDateFormat("MMMM dd, yyyy", Locale.getDefault())

    fun buildCompleteArchiveText(
        profileName: String,
        profileAge: Int,
        profilePhotoUri: String,
        currentWeek: Int,
        dueDateMillis: Long,
        isTwinMode: Boolean,
        userEmail: String,
        pregnancyLogs: List<PregnancyLogEntity>,
        kickSessions: List<KickSessionEntity>,
        babyLogs: List<BabyLogEntity>,
        journalEntries: List<JournalEntryEntity>,
        appointments: List<AppointmentEntity>,
        checklistItems: List<ChecklistItemEntity>,
        medicalContacts: List<MedicalContactEntity>
    ): String {
        val sb = StringBuilder()
        val border = "================================================================================"
        val subBorder = "--------------------------------------------------------------------------------"

        val nowFormatted = dateFormat.format(Date())
        val dueDateFormatted = dateOnlyFormat.format(Date(dueDateMillis))
        val daysRemaining = ((dueDateMillis - System.currentTimeMillis()) / (1000L * 3600 * 24)).coerceAtLeast(0)

        sb.appendLine(border)
        sb.appendLine("                      MAMAN & BÉBÉ - COMPLETE DATA ARCHIVE")
        sb.appendLine("           Personal Pregnancy, Postpartum, & Clinical Care Records")
        sb.appendLine(border)
        sb.appendLine("Generated: $nowFormatted")
        sb.appendLine("Application: Maman & Bébé (Android)")
        sb.appendLine()

        // 1. User Profile & Clinical Overview
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        sb.appendLine("1. PATIENT & MATERNAL PROFILE")
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        sb.appendLine("Name:                $profileName")
        sb.appendLine("Age:                 $profileAge years old")
        sb.appendLine("Registered Account:  $userEmail")
        sb.appendLine("Current Gestation:   Week $currentWeek")
        sb.appendLine("Estimated Due Date:  $dueDateFormatted")
        sb.appendLine("Countdown:           $daysRemaining days until expected arrival")
        sb.appendLine("Pregnancy Type:      ${if (isTwinMode) "Twin Pregnancy (Multiples)" else "Single Pregnancy"}")
        if (profilePhotoUri.isNotBlank()) {
            sb.appendLine("Profile Photo URI:   $profilePhotoUri")
        }
        sb.appendLine()

        // 2. Scheduled Prenatal Appointments & Clinical Care Visits
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        sb.appendLine("2. PRENATAL APPOINTMENTS & MEDICAL CLINICAL AGENDA")
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        if (appointments.isEmpty()) {
            sb.appendLine("No clinical appointments recorded.")
        } else {
            appointments.sortedBy { it.dateTimestamp }.forEachIndexed { idx, appt ->
                val apptDate = dateFormat.format(Date(appt.dateTimestamp))
                val statusStr = if (appt.isCompleted) "[COMPLETED]" else "[UPCOMING / SCHEDULED]"
                sb.appendLine("#${idx + 1} $statusStr: ${appt.title}")
                sb.appendLine("   Date & Time:      $apptDate")
                if (appt.doctorName.isNotBlank()) sb.appendLine("   Doctor/Provider:  ${appt.doctorName}")
                if (appt.location.isNotBlank())   sb.appendLine("   Location/Clinic:  ${appt.location}")
                if (appt.notes.isNotBlank())      sb.appendLine("   Clinical Notes:   ${appt.notes}")
                sb.appendLine()
            }
        }

        // 3. Care Directory & Emergency Contacts
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        sb.appendLine("3. PRENATAL CARE DIRECTORY & CONTACTS")
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        if (medicalContacts.isEmpty()) {
            sb.appendLine("No contacts recorded.")
        } else {
            medicalContacts.forEachIndexed { idx, contact ->
                sb.appendLine("#${idx + 1} [${contact.type}] ${contact.name}")
                if (contact.phone.isNotBlank())   sb.appendLine("   Phone:   ${contact.phone}")
                if (contact.address.isNotBlank()) sb.appendLine("   Address: ${contact.address}")
                if (contact.notes.isNotBlank())   sb.appendLine("   Notes:   ${contact.notes}")
                sb.appendLine()
            }
        }

        // 4. Daily Health Logs (Weight, Hydration, Symptoms, Blood Pressure, Mood)
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        sb.appendLine("4. DAILY HEALTH, SYMPTOM & HYDRATION LOGS")
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        if (pregnancyLogs.isEmpty()) {
            sb.appendLine("No daily health logs recorded.")
        } else {
            pregnancyLogs.sortedByDescending { it.timestamp }.forEachIndexed { idx, log ->
                val logDate = dateFormat.format(Date(log.timestamp))
                sb.appendLine("#${idx + 1} Date: ${log.dateString} ($logDate)")
                sb.appendLine("   Mood:            ${log.mood}")
                sb.appendLine("   Weight:          ${log.weightKg} kg")
                sb.appendLine("   Water Intake:    ${log.waterGlasses} glasses (approx. ${log.waterGlasses * 250} ml)")
                if (log.bloodPressure.isNotBlank()) sb.appendLine("   Blood Pressure:  ${log.bloodPressure}")
                if (log.symptoms.isNotBlank())      sb.appendLine("   Symptoms:        ${log.symptoms}")
                if (log.notes.isNotBlank())         sb.appendLine("   Personal Notes:  ${log.notes}")
                sb.appendLine()
            }
        }

        // 5. Fetal Movement & Kick Counter Sessions
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        sb.appendLine("5. FETAL MOVEMENT & KICK COUNTER SESSIONS")
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        if (kickSessions.isEmpty()) {
            sb.appendLine("No kick counter sessions recorded.")
        } else {
            kickSessions.sortedByDescending { it.timestamp }.forEachIndexed { idx, kick ->
                val kickDate = dateFormat.format(Date(kick.timestamp))
                val mins = kick.durationSeconds / 60
                val secs = kick.durationSeconds % 60
                val status = if (kick.targetReached) "Goal Reached (10 kicks)" else "Session Incomplete"
                sb.appendLine("#${idx + 1} Session Date: $kickDate")
                sb.appendLine("   Total Kicks:    ${kick.kickCount} kicks")
                sb.appendLine("   Duration:       ${mins}m ${secs}s")
                sb.appendLine("   Outcome:        $status")
                if (kick.notes.isNotBlank()) sb.appendLine("   Notes:          ${kick.notes}")
                sb.appendLine()
            }
        }

        // 6. Milestone Journal & Memory Reflections
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        sb.appendLine("6. MILESTONE JOURNAL & PREGNANCY REFLECTIONS")
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        if (journalEntries.isEmpty()) {
            sb.appendLine("No journal entries recorded.")
        } else {
            journalEntries.sortedBy { it.weekNumber }.forEachIndexed { idx, entry ->
                sb.appendLine("#${idx + 1} Week ${entry.weekNumber}: ${entry.title}")
                sb.appendLine("   Category:   ${entry.category}")
                sb.appendLine("   Date:       ${entry.dateString}")
                if (!entry.photoUri.isNullOrBlank()) sb.appendLine("   Photo URI:  ${entry.photoUri}")
                sb.appendLine("   Reflection: ${entry.reflection}")
                sb.appendLine()
            }
        }

        // 7. Postpartum Baby Care Logs
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        sb.appendLine("7. POSTPARTUM BABY CARE LOGS")
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        if (babyLogs.isEmpty()) {
            sb.appendLine("No baby care logs recorded.")
        } else {
            babyLogs.sortedByDescending { it.timestamp }.forEachIndexed { idx, baby ->
                val babyDate = dateFormat.format(Date(baby.timestamp))
                sb.appendLine("#${idx + 1} [${baby.logType}] $babyDate")
                sb.appendLine("   Details:   ${baby.detail}")
                if (baby.amountMl > 0) sb.appendLine("   Volume:    ${baby.amountMl} ml")
                if (baby.durationSeconds > 0) {
                    val mins = baby.durationSeconds / 60
                    sb.appendLine("   Duration:  ${mins} minutes")
                }
                if (baby.notes.isNotBlank()) sb.appendLine("   Notes:     ${baby.notes}")
                sb.appendLine()
            }
        }

        // 8. Preparation & Hospital Bag Checklists
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        sb.appendLine("8. PREPARATION & HOSPITAL BAG CHECKLISTS")
        sb.appendLine(subBorder)
        if (checklistItems.isEmpty()) {
            sb.appendLine("No checklist items recorded.")
        } else {
            val grouped = checklistItems.groupBy { it.category }
            grouped.forEach { (cat, items) ->
                sb.appendLine("[$cat]")
                items.forEach { item ->
                    val checkMark = if (item.isChecked) "[X]" else "[ ]"
                    sb.appendLine("  $checkMark ${item.title}")
                }
                sb.appendLine()
            }
        }

        sb.appendLine(border)
        sb.appendLine("END OF MAMAN & BÉBÉ ARCHIVE EXPORT")
        sb.appendLine(border)

        return sb.toString()
    }

    /**
     * Saves the formatted text content into the app's cache exports directory as a .txt file.
     */
    fun saveArchiveToFile(context: Context, textContent: String, currentWeek: Int): File? {
        return try {
            val exportDir = File(context.cacheDir, "exports")
            if (!exportDir.exists()) exportDir.mkdirs()
            val txtFile = File(exportDir, "Maman_Bebe_Complete_Archive_Week_${currentWeek}.txt")
            val outputStream = FileOutputStream(txtFile)
            outputStream.write(textContent.toByteArray(Charsets.UTF_8))
            outputStream.flush()
            outputStream.close()
            txtFile
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            null
        }
    }

    /**
     * Shares the exported text file using Android's standard share intent sheet.
     */
    fun shareTextFile(context: Context, txtFile: File) {
        try {
            val uri = FileProvider.getUriForFile(
                context,
                "${context.packageName}.fileprovider",
                txtFile
            )

            val intent = Intent(Intent.ACTION_SEND).apply {
                type = "text/plain"
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_STREAM, uri)
                putExtra(Intent.EXTRA_SUBJECT, "Maman & Bébé Complete Pregnancy & Postpartum Archive")
                putExtra(
                    Intent.EXTRA_TEXT,
                    "Here is my complete pregnancy and postpartum archive exported from Maman & Bébé."
                )
                addFlags(Intent.FLAG_GRANT_READ_URI_PERMISSION)
            }

            context.startActivity(Intent.createChooser(intent, "Share Complete Archive (.txt) via"))
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            Toast.makeText(context, "Failed to share archive: ${e.localizedMessage}", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }

    /**
     * Copies the full plain text export to clipboard.
     */
    fun copyToClipboard(context: Context, textContent: String) {
        try {
            val clipboard = context.getSystemService(Context.CLIPBOARD_SERVICE) as ClipboardManager
            val clip = ClipData.newPlainText("Maman & Bébé Complete Archive", textContent)
            clipboard.setPrimaryClip(clip)
            Toast.makeText(context, "Complete archive copied to clipboard!", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        } catch (e: Exception) {
            e.printStackTrace()
            Toast.makeText(context, "Could not copy to clipboard", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
        }
    }
}

FILE 70/77: app/src/test/java/com/example/ExampleRobolectricTest.kt
================================================================================

package com.example

import android.content.Context
import androidx.test.core.app.ApplicationProvider
import com.example.data.PregnancyWeeksData
import com.example.health.HealthConnectManager
import org.junit.Assert.assertEquals
import org.junit.Assert.assertNotNull
import org.junit.Assert.assertTrue
import org.junit.Test
import org.junit.runner.RunWith
import org.robolectric.RobolectricTestRunner
import org.robolectric.annotation.Config

@RunWith(RobolectricTestRunner::class)
@Config(sdk = [34])
class ExampleRobolectricTest {

    @Test
    fun `read string from context`() {
        val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()
        val appName = context.getString(R.string.app_name)
        assertEquals("Maman & bébé Pregnancy Tracker", appName)
    }

    @Test
    fun `verify pregnancy weeks data coverage`() {
        val week24 = PregnancyWeeksData.getWeek(24)
        assertNotNull(week24)
        assertEquals(24, week24.weekNumber)
        assertEquals(2, week24.trimester)
        assertTrue(week24.fruitAnalogy.isNotBlank())
    }

    @Test
    fun `verify health connect permissions declared`() {
        val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<Context>()
        val manager = HealthConnectManager(context)
        assertTrue(manager.permissions.isNotEmpty())
        assertTrue(manager.permissions.any { it.contains("weight", ignoreCase = true) })
        assertTrue(manager.permissions.any { it.contains("sleep", ignoreCase = true) })
    }

    @Test
    fun `verify database and pregnancy viewmodel initialize without crash`() {
        val context = ApplicationProvider.getApplicationContext<android.app.Application>()
        val db = com.example.data.local.AppDatabase.getInstance(context)
        assertNotNull(db)
        val dao = db.pregnancyDao()
        assertNotNull(dao)

        val vm = com.example.ui.viewmodel.PregnancyViewModel(context)
        assertNotNull(vm)
        assertNotNull(vm.currentTab.value)
        assertNotNull(vm.currentWeekNumber.value)
    }

    @Test
    fun `verify MainActivity launches cleanly`() {
        val controller = org.robolectric.Robolectric.buildActivity(MainActivity::class.java).setup()
        assertNotNull(controller.get())
    }
}

FILE 75/77: firestore.rules
================================================================================

rules_version = '2';
service cloud.firestore {
  match /databases/{database}/documents {
    
    function isAuthenticated() {
      return request.auth != null;
    }

    function isOwner(userId) {
      return isAuthenticated() && request.auth.uid == userId;
    }

    match /users/{userId} {
      allow read, write: if isOwner(userId);

      match /journalEntries/{entryId} {
        allow read, write: if isOwner(userId);
      }

      match /appointments/{appointmentId} {
        allow read, write: if isOwner(userId);
      }

      match /cachedContacts/{contactId} {
        allow read, write: if isOwner(userId);
      }
    }
  }
}
